    63	
    64	export function parseAntigravityOutput(stdout: string): ProviderParsedOutput {
    65	    const envelope = parseEnvelope(stdout);
    66	
    67	    if (envelope.status && envelope.status !== 'SUCCESS') {
    68	        throw new Error(`Antigravity CLI reported status ${envelope.status}.`);
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    const result =
    72	        envelope.structured_output ??
    73	        (typeof envelope.response === 'string' ? tryParseJson(envelope.response) : null);
    74	
    75	    if (result === null || result === undefined) {
    76	        throw new Error(
    77	            'Antigravity CLI output contains no structured result. Check that the model finished the task (auth, quota, timeout).',
    78	        );
    79	    }
    80	
    81	    return {
    82	        result,
    83	        meta: {
    84	            conversationId: envelope.conversation_id ?? null,
    85	            durationSeconds: envelope.duration_seconds ?? null,
    86	            usage: envelope.usage ?? null,
    87	        },
    88	    };
    89	}
    90	
    91	function parseEnvelope(stdout: string): AgyPrintEnvelope {
    92	    const trimmed = stdout.trim();
    93	    let parsed = tryParseJson(trimmed);
    94	
    95	    if (parsed === null) {
    96	        const firstBrace = trimmed.indexOf('{');
    97	        const lastBrace = trimmed.lastIndexOf('}');
    98	        if (firstBrace >= 0 && lastBrace > firstBrace) {
    99	            parsed = tryParseJson(trimmed.slice(firstBrace, lastBrace + 1));
   100	        }
   101	    }
   102	
   103	    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object') {
   104	        throw new Error('Failed to parse Antigravity CLI JSON output.');
   105	    }
   106	
   107	    return parsed as AgyPrintEnvelope;
   108	}
   109	
   110	function tryParseJson(text: string): unknown | null {
   111	    try {
   112	        return JSON.parse(text);
   113	    } catch {
   114	        return null;
   115	    }
   116	}
   117	
   118	const SWITCH_HINT = `Or switch to a provider with its own quota and no interactive login:
   119	  modlens config set gemini-api.apiKey <key>   # free key, no card: https://aistudio.google.com
   120	  modlens config set provider gemini-api`;
   121	
   122	/**
   123	 * agy exits 1 for auth and quota problems alike, and its own envelope says
   124	 * only "Agent execution terminated due to error." The distinguishing evidence
   125	 * lives in agy's log file, so read it before guessing (issue #3).
   126	 */
   127	export function describeAntigravityFailure(context: ProviderFailureContext): string | null {
   128	    // Only lines written after this run started can describe this run. A
   129	    // two-minute window alone let a previous quota failure, or a concurrent
   130	    // agy call, misdiagnose an unrelated error.
   131	    const since = context.startedAt ?? Date.now() - LOG_FRESHNESS_MS;
   132	    let envelope: AgyPrintEnvelope | null = null;
   133	    try {
   134	        envelope = parseEnvelope(context.stdout);
   135	    } catch {
   136	        envelope = null;
   137	    }
   138	    // No agy envelope means agy never ran (missing binary, wrapper, crash), so
   139	    // this is not an agy-level error and its logs say nothing about it.
   140	    if (!envelope) {
   141	        return null;
   142	    }
   143	    const agyError = typeof envelope?.error === 'string' ? envelope.error.trim() : '';
   144	    const evidence = `${agyError}\n${context.stderr}\n${readRecentAgyLog(since)}`.toLowerCase();
   145	
   146	    if (evidence.includes('quota')) {
   147	        return [
   148	            agyError || 'Antigravity CLI reported a quota error.',
   149	            "agy's free tier is one weekly bucket shared by the desktop app, the CLI, and the SDK, and subagents drain it in parallel. Wait for the reset shown above, or use a different provider.",
   150	            SWITCH_HINT,
   151	        ].join('\n\n');
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    if (
   155	        evidence.includes('not logged into antigravity') ||
   156	        evidence.includes('getting token source') ||
   157	        evidence.includes('keyring') ||
   158	        evidence.includes('failed to read token store')
   159	    ) {
   160	        return [
   161	            'Antigravity CLI cannot read its stored login token.',
   162	            "On Linux this usually means the OS keyring is locked, which is normal for headless sessions (agents, cron, systemd, SSH without a desktop login). agy then reports it as being signed out and tries a browser sign-in that cannot complete without a display. Unlock the keyring, or run modlens from a desktop session, or sign in again with `agy`.",
   163	            SWITCH_HINT,
   164	        ].join('\n\n');
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    const totalTokens = (envelope?.usage as { total_tokens?: number } | undefined)?.total_tokens;
   168	    if (agyError || totalTokens === 0) {
   169	        return [
   170	            agyError || 'Antigravity CLI exited before doing any work (no tokens consumed).',
   171	            `Usually auth or quota. Check \`agy\` interactively, and look at the newest log in ${agyLogDir()} for the real reason.`,
   172	            SWITCH_HINT,
   173	        ].join('\n\n');
   174	    }
   175	
   176	    return null;
   177	}
   178	
   179	function agyLogDir(): string {
   180	    return path.join(os.homedir(), '.gemini', 'antigravity-cli', 'log');
   181	}
   182	
   183	// Older logs belong to earlier runs and would misdiagnose this one.
   184	const LOG_FRESHNESS_MS = 2 * 60 * 1000;
   185	
   186	/** Tail of agy's newest log file when it belongs to this run, else empty. */
   187	function readRecentAgyLog(since: number): string {
   188	    try {
   189	        const dir = agyLogDir();
   190	        const newest = fs
   191	            .readdirSync(dir)
   192	            .filter((name) => name.endsWith('.log'))
   193	            .map((name) => {
   194	                const full = path.join(dir, name);
   195	                return { full, mtime: fs.statSync(full).mtimeMs };
   196	            })
   197	            .sort((a, b) => b.mtime - a.mtime)[0];
   198	        // The file must have been touched during this run, not merely recently.
   199	        if (!newest || newest.mtime < since) {
   200	            return '';
   201	        }
   202	        // Only the tail matters, and these files can get large.
   203	        return fs.readFileSync(newest.full, 'utf-8').slice(-8000);
   204	    } catch {
   205	        return '';
   206	    }
   207	}
   208	
   209	export const antigravityCliProvider: VisionProvider = {
   210	    name: 'antigravity-cli',
   211	    defaultModel: DEFAULT_MODEL,
   212	    buildInvocation: buildAntigravityInvocation,
   213	    parseOutput: parseAntigravityOutput,
   214	    describeFailure: describeAntigravityFailure,
   215	    hasInternalTimeout: true,
   216	};

    62	export function parseClaudeCliOutput(stdout: string): ProviderParsedOutput {
    63	    const envelope = parseEnvelope(stdout);
    64	
    65	    if (envelope.is_error || (envelope.subtype && envelope.subtype !== 'success')) {
    66	        throw new Error(
    67	            `Claude CLI reported ${envelope.subtype ?? 'an error'}: ${truncate(envelope.result ?? '')}`,
    68	        );
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    if (typeof envelope.result !== 'string' || !envelope.result.trim()) {
    72	        throw new Error('Claude CLI output contains no result. Check login state (run: claude).');
    73	    }
    74	
    75	    let result: unknown;
    76	    try {
    77	        result = JSON.parse(envelope.result);
    78	    } catch {
    79	        throw new Error(`Claude CLI returned non-JSON result: ${truncate(envelope.result)}`);
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    return {
    83	        result,
    84	        meta: {
    85	            conversationId: envelope.session_id ?? null,
    86	            durationSeconds:
    87	                typeof envelope.duration_ms === 'number' ? envelope.duration_ms / 1000 : null,
    88	            usage: envelope.usage ?? null,
    89	        },
    90	    };
    91	}
    92	
    93	function parseEnvelope(stdout: string): ClaudePrintEnvelope {
    94	    const trimmed = stdout.trim();
    95	    let parsed = tryParseJson(trimmed);
    96	
    97	    if (parsed === null) {
    98	        const firstBrace = trimmed.indexOf('{');
    99	        const lastBrace = trimmed.lastIndexOf('}');
   100	        if (firstBrace >= 0 && lastBrace > firstBrace) {
   101	            parsed = tryParseJson(trimmed.slice(firstBrace, lastBrace + 1));
   102	        }
   103	    }
   104	
   105	    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object') {
   106	        throw new Error('Failed to parse Claude CLI JSON output.');
   107	    }
   108	
   109	    return parsed as ClaudePrintEnvelope;
   110	}
   111	
   112	function tryParseJson(text: string): unknown | null {
   113	    try {
   114	        return JSON.parse(text);
   115	    } catch {
   116	        return null;
   117	    }
   118	}

   120	function truncate(text: string): string {
   121	    return text.length > 300 ? `${text.slice(0, 300)}...` : text;
   122	}

     1	// OpenAI-compatible provider: works with any /chat/completions endpoint that
     2	// accepts image_url content (DashScope qwen-vl, OpenAI, GLM, ...). No portable
     3	// schema enforcement across vendors, so the schema rides in the prompt and the
     4	// response goes through tolerant JSON extraction.
     5	import { extractJson, readLocalImageBase64 } from '../imageInput.ts';
     6	import { buildVisionPrompt } from '../prompt.ts';
     7	import type {
     8	    BuildProviderInvocationOptions,
     9	    ProviderParsedOutput,
    10	    VisionProvider,
    11	} from './index.ts';
    12	
    13	export async function executeOpenaiCompat(
    14	    options: BuildProviderInvocationOptions,
    15	): Promise<ProviderParsedOutput> {
    16	    const apiKey = options.settings?.apiKey;
    17	    const baseUrl = options.settings?.baseUrl?.replace(/\/$/, '');
    18	    const model = options.model || options.settings?.model;
    19

    21	        throw new Error(
    22	            'openai provider needs baseUrl, apiKey, and model. Set OPENAI_BASE_URL and OPENAI_API_KEY, or run: modlens config set openai.baseUrl <url> / openai.apiKey <key> / openai.model <name>',
    23	        );

    32	    // echo a schema back instead of instantiating it.

    34	        imageSource: options.imageSource,
    35	        imageKind: 'inline',
    36	        extraPrompt: options.extraPrompt,

    40	{"summary":"one paragraph describing the image","ocr":{"full_text":"all visible text","lines":[{"text":"one line","language":"en"}]},"layout":{"regions":[{"type":"title|subtitle|paragraph|list|table|chart|form|code|image|icon|other","reading_order":1,"text":"region text"}]},"semantics":{"scene":"what kind of scene","intent":"what the image is for","entities":[{"name":"entity","type":"kind","evidence":"where seen"}],"relations":[{"subject":"a","predicate":"relates to","object":"b"}]},"visual":{"dominant_colors":["color"],"style":"visual style","notes":["notable visual detail"]},"uncertainty":["anything unreadable or ambiguous"]}`;

    55	                        { type: 'image_url', image_url: { url: imageUrl } },
    56	                        { type: 'text', text: prompt },

    58	                },
    59	            ],
    60	        }),
    61	        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(options.timeoutMs),
    62	    });
    63	
    64	    if (!response.ok) {
    65	        const body = await response.text();
    66	        throw new Error(`OpenAI-compatible API error ${response.status}: ${truncate(body)}`);
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    const payload = (await response.json()) as {
    70	        choices?: Array<{ message?: { content?: string } }>;
    71	        usage?: unknown;
    72	    };
    73	
    74	    const text = payload.choices?.[0]?.message?.content;
    75	    if (!text) {
    76	        throw new Error('OpenAI-compatible API returned no message content.');
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    const result = extractJson(text);
    80	    if (result === null) {
    81	        throw new Error(`OpenAI-compatible API returned non-JSON output: ${truncate(text)}`);
    82	    }
    83	    // No portable server-side schema enforcement on this route, so verify the
    84	    // shape instead of silently returning something that only looks right.
    85	    // A token check let {"summary":"x","ocr":null} through, along with results
    86	    // missing every other required field.
    87	    const shaped = result as Record<string, unknown>;
    88	    const missing = ['summary', 'ocr', 'layout', 'semantics', 'visual', 'uncertainty'].filter(
    89	        (field) => shaped[field] === undefined || shaped[field] === null,
    90	    );
    91	    if (
    92	        missing.length > 0 ||
    93	        typeof shaped.summary !== 'string' ||
    94	        typeof shaped.ocr !== 'object' ||
    95	        !Array.isArray(shaped.uncertainty)
    96	    ) {
    97	        throw new Error(
    98	            `OpenAI-compatible API returned JSON that does not match the vision schema${missing.length > 0 ? ` (missing: ${missing.join(', ')})` : ''}. Retry, or switch to gemini-api / anthropic for enforced schemas. Got: ${truncate(text)}`,
    99	        );
   100	    }
   101	
   102	    return {
   103	        result,
   104	        meta: {
   105	            conversationId: null,
   106	            durationSeconds: (Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000,
   107	            usage: payload.usage ?? null,
   108	        },
   109	    };
   110	}
   111	
   112	function toDataUrl(image: { data: string; mimeType: string }): string {
   113	    return `data:${image.mimeType};base64,${image.data}`;
   114	}

   117	function truncate(text: string): string {
   118	    return text.length > 300 ? `${text.slice(0, 300)}...` : text;
   119	}

   258	export function piSessionSlug(cwd: string): string {
   259	    const resolved = path.resolve(cwd);

   263	function piExtractLine(line: unknown): ImageBlockRef[] {
   264	    const message = (line as { message?: { role?: string; content?: unknown } }).message;
   265	    if (message?.role !== 'user' || !Array.isArray(message.content)) {
   266	        return [];
   267	    }
   268	    const images: ImageBlockRef[] = [];
   269	    for (const block of message.content) {
   270	        const typed = block as { type?: string; data?: string; mimeType?: string };
   271	        if (typed?.type === 'image' && typed.data) {
   272	            images.push({ mediaType: typed.mimeType ?? 'image/png', data: typed.data });
   273	        }
   274	    }
   275	    return images;
   276	}

   291	
   292	/** LIKE reads _ and % as wildcards, so a literal path must escape them. */
   293	export function escapeLikePattern(value: string): string {
   294	    return value.replace(/[\\%_]/g, (char) => `\\${char}`);
   295	}
   296	
   297	interface SqliteRow {
   298	    data: string;
   299	    time_created: number;
   300	    session_id: string;
   301	}
   302	
   303	function opencodeQuery(dbPath: string, cwd: string, sessionId?: string): SqliteRow[] {
   304	    // node:sqlite ships with Node 22.5+. Loaded lazily so the other adapters
   305	    // keep working on older runtimes.
   306	    let DatabaseSync: new (p: string, o?: { readOnly?: boolean }) => {
   307	        prepare: (sql: string) => { all: (...params: unknown[]) => unknown[] };
   308	        close: () => void;
   309	    };
   310	    try {
   311	        const nodeRequire = createRequire(import.meta.url);
   312	        ({ DatabaseSync } = nodeRequire('node:sqlite'));
   313	    } catch {
   314	        throw new Error(
   315	            'Reading opencode storage needs the node:sqlite module (Node 22.5+). Upgrade Node, or pass --transcript/--session for a JSONL-based harness.',
   316	        );
   317	    }
   318	
   319	    const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath, { readOnly: true });
   320	    try {
   321	        const resolved = path.resolve(cwd);
   322	        // opencode's bash tool runs at the repo root while session.directory
   323	        // records where the session was launched (possibly a subdirectory), so
   324	        // directories must match by prefix in both directions, not exactly.
   325	        //
   326	        // Two traps live here. LIKE treats _ and % as wildcards, so a project
   327	        // path containing either matched other projects until escaped. And a
   328	        // session id or slug is not unique across projects, so it narrows the
   329	        // directory match rather than replacing it.
   330	        const directoryFilter = `(session.directory = ? OR session.directory LIKE ? || '/%' ESCAPE '\\' OR ? LIKE session.directory || '/%' ESCAPE '\\')`;
   331	        const escaped = escapeLikePattern(resolved);
   332	        const sessionFilter = sessionId
   333	            ? `AND ${directoryFilter} AND (session.id = ? OR session.slug = ?)`
   334	            : `AND ${directoryFilter}`;
   335	        const params = sessionId
   336	            ? [resolved, escaped, resolved, sessionId, sessionId]
   337	            : [resolved, escaped, resolved];
   338	        const rows = db
   339	            .prepare(
   340	                `SELECT part.data AS data, part.time_created AS time_created, part.session_id AS session_id
   341	                 FROM part
   342	                 JOIN message ON message.id = part.message_id
   343	                 JOIN session ON session.id = part.session_id
   344	                 WHERE part.data LIKE '{"type":"file"%'
   345	                   AND json_extract(message.data, '$.role') = 'user'
   346	                   ${sessionFilter}
   347	                 ORDER BY part.time_created ASC`,
   348	            )
   349	            .all(...params) as SqliteRow[];
   350	        return rows;
   351	    } finally {
   352	        db.close();
   353	    }
   354	}
   355	
   356	function opencodeImagesFromRows(rows: SqliteRow[]): ImageBlockRef[] {
   357	    const images: ImageBlockRef[] = [];
   358	    for (const row of rows) {
   359	        try {
   360	            const part = JSON.parse(row.data) as {
   361	                type?: string;
   362	                mime?: string;
   363	                url?: string;
   364	                filename?: string;
   365	            };
   366	            if (part.type !== 'file' || !part.mime?.startsWith('image/')) {
   367	                continue;
   368	            }
   369	            const match = /^data:[^;]+;base64,(.+)$/.exec(part.url ?? '');
   370	            if (match) {
   371	                images.push({ mediaType: part.mime, data: match[1], filename: part.filename });
   372	            }
   373	        } catch {
   374	            // skip malformed parts
   375	        }
   376	    }
   377	    return images;
   378	}

   380	const opencodeAdapter: HarnessAdapter = {
   381	    name: 'opencode',
   382	    describe: () => opencodeDbPath(),
   383	    findNewest: (cwd) => {
   384	        const dbPath = opencodeDbPath();
   385	        if (!fs.existsSync(dbPath)) {
   386	            return null;
   387	        }
   388	        // One source = one session, like the JSONL adapters: scope to the
   389	        // session owning the newest image part, so other sessions in the same
   390	        // project cannot smuggle extra images into the result.
   391	        const withImages = opencodeQuery(dbPath, cwd)
   392	            .map((row) => ({ row, images: opencodeImagesFromRows([row]) }))
   393	            .filter((entry) => entry.images.length > 0);
   394	        if (withImages.length === 0) {
   395	            return null;
   396	        }
   397	        const newest = withImages[withImages.length - 1];
   398	        const scoped = withImages.filter((entry) => entry.row.session_id === newest.row.session_id);
   399	        return {
   400	            ref: {
   401	                harness: 'opencode',
   402	                location: dbPath,
   403	                extract: () => scoped.flatMap((entry) => entry.images),
   404	            },
   405	            timestamp: newest.row.time_created,
   406	        };
   407	    },
   408	    findSession: (cwd, sessionId) => {
   409	        const dbPath = opencodeDbPath();
   410	        if (!fs.existsSync(dbPath)) {
   411	            return null;
   412	        }
   413	        const rows = opencodeQuery(dbPath, cwd, sessionId);
   414	        if (opencodeImagesFromRows(rows).length === 0) {
   415	            return null;
   416	        }
   417	        return {
   418	            harness: 'opencode',
   419	            location: dbPath,
   420	            extract: () => opencodeImagesFromRows(opencodeQuery(dbPath, cwd, sessionId)),
   421	        };
   422	    },
   423	};
   424	
   425	// ---------- harness detection ----------
   426	//
   427	// Recovery must read the storage of the harness this command is actually
   428	// running inside; racing every store by timestamp lets one tool's stale
   429	// sessions hijack another tool's paste. The primary signal is the process
   430	// ancestry: the nearest known harness among our parent processes is the one
   431	// that spawned this command, which also resolves nested setups (opencode
   432	// launched from inside Claude Code) to the innermost harness, the one whose
   433	// input box received the paste. Env fingerprints are the fallback: Claude Code
   434	// injects CLAUDECODE/CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID, pi sets PI_CODING_AGENT, codex
   435	// injects CODEX_THREAD_ID; opencode injects nothing, which is exactly why
   436	// ancestry comes first.
   437	
   438	const HARNESS_BY_BASENAME: Record<string, string> = {
   439	    claude: 'claude-code',
   440	    'claude-code': 'claude-code',
   441	    pi: 'pi',
   442	    opencode: 'opencode',
   443	    codex: 'codex',
   444	};
   445	
   446	/** Walk the ppid chain in a `ps -Ao pid=,ppid=,command=` table, nearest first. */
   447	export function harnessFromPsTable(psOutput: string, startPid: number): string | null {
   448	    const table = new Map<number, { ppid: number; command: string }>();
   449	    for (const line of psOutput.split('\n')) {
   450	        const match = /^\s*(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(.+)$/.exec(line);
   451	        if (match) {
   452	            table.set(Number(match[1]), { ppid: Number(match[2]), command: match[3] });
   453	        }
   454	    }
   455	    let pid: number | undefined = table.get(startPid)?.ppid;
   456	    for (let hops = 0; hops < 50 && pid !== undefined && pid > 1; hops++) {
   457	        const proc = table.get(pid);
   458	        if (!proc) {
   459	            return null;
   460	        }
   461	        // Only the executable itself, plus the script path when it is run
   462	        // through a node shim. Scanning further matched plain arguments: a
   463	        // command that merely mentioned "pi" was detected as Pi.
   464	        const tokens = proc.command.trim().split(/\s+/);
   465	        const candidates = [tokens[0]];
   466	        if (/^(node|bun|deno)$/.test(path.basename(tokens[0] ?? ''))) {
   467	            const script = tokens.slice(1).find((token) => !token.startsWith('-'));
   468	            if (script) {
   469	                candidates.push(script);
   470	            }
   471	        }
   472	        for (const token of candidates) {
   473	            const mapped = token ? HARNESS_BY_BASENAME[path.basename(token)] : undefined;
   474	            if (mapped) {
   475	                return mapped;
   476	            }
   477	        }
   478	        pid = proc.ppid;
   479	    }
   480	    return null;
   481	}
   482	
   483	function detectHarness(): string | null {
   484	    const override = process.env.MODLENS_HARNESS;
   485	    if (override) {
   486	        return override === 'none' ? null : override;
   487	    }
   488	    try {
   489	        const ps = childProcess.execFileSync('ps', ['-Ao', 'pid=,ppid=,command='], {
   490	            encoding: 'utf-8',
   491	            maxBuffer: 16 * 1024 * 1024,
   492	        });
   493	        const found = harnessFromPsTable(ps, process.pid);
   494	        if (found) {
   495	            return found;
   496	        }
   497	    } catch {
   498	        // ps unavailable (e.g. Windows): fall through to env fingerprints
   499	    }
   500	    if (process.env.PI_CODING_AGENT) {
   501	        return 'pi';
   502	    }
   503	    if (process.env.CODEX_THREAD_ID || process.env.CODEX_SANDBOX) {
   504	        return 'codex';
   505	    }
   506	    if (process.env.CLAUDECODE || process.env.CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID) {
   507	        return 'claude-code';
   508	    }
   509	    return null;
   510	}

   520	    if (declared === 'opencode' || (!declared && filePath.endsWith('.db'))) {

   130	describe('locateTranscript (via recoverPastedImages)', () => {
   131	    it('picks the session with the newest image timestamp, resisting mtime misdirection', () => {
   132	        const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-home-'));
   133	        const cwd = '/tmp/proj';
   134	        const dir = path.join(home, '.claude', 'projects', '-tmp-proj');
   135	        fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   136	
   137	        // session a: older image, but file touched later (concurrent activity)
   138	        fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'a.jsonl'), imageLine('old-image', '2026-08-03T01:00:00.000Z'));
   139	        // session b: the actual paste, newer image timestamp, older mtime
   140	        fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'b.jsonl'), imageLine('new-image', '2026-08-03T02:00:00.000Z'));
   141	        const past = new Date(Date.now() - 60_000);
   142	        fs.utimesSync(path.join(dir, 'b.jsonl'), past, past);
   143	
   144	        const realHome = process.env.HOME;
   145	        process.env.HOME = home;
   146	        try {
   147	            const result = recoverPastedImages({ cwd, outDir: path.join(home, 'out') });
   148	            expect(result.transcript.endsWith('b.jsonl')).toBe(true);
   149	            expect(fs.readFileSync(result.images[0].path).toString()).toBe('new-image');
   150	        } finally {
   151	            process.env.HOME = realHome;
   152	            fs.rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });
   153	        }
   154	    });
   155	});

   157	describe('transcriptForSession', () => {
   158	    it('targets the exact session file and errors on a missing one', () => {

   166	        );

   169	            path.join(dir, 'cccc-dddd.jsonl'),

   184	                'No session missing-id with pasted images',

   186	        } finally {

   188	            fs.rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });
   189	        }

   251	describe('opencode harness support', async () => {
   252	    const { recoverPastedImages: recover } = await import('./recoverPaste.ts');
   253	    const { createRequire } = await import('module');
   254	    const nodeRequire = createRequire(import.meta.url);
   255	    const { DatabaseSync } = nodeRequire('node:sqlite');
   256	
   257	    function openDb(home: string) {
   258	        const dir = path.join(home, '.local', 'share', 'opencode');
   259	        fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   260	        const db = new DatabaseSync(path.join(dir, 'opencode.db'));
   261	        db.exec(`
   262	            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS session (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, slug TEXT, directory TEXT);
   263	            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS message (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, session_id TEXT, time_created INTEGER, data TEXT);
   264	            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS part (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, message_id TEXT, session_id TEXT, time_created INTEGER, data TEXT);
   265	        `);
   266	        return db;
   267	    }
   268	
   269	    function insertImage(
   270	        db: InstanceType<typeof DatabaseSync>,
   271	        n: number,
   272	        slug: string,
   273	        directory: string,
   274	        timeMs: number,
   275	        payload: string,
   276	    ) {
   277	        db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO session VALUES (?, ?, ?)').run(`ses_${n}`, slug, directory);
   278	        db.prepare(`INSERT INTO message VALUES (?, ?, ?, '{"role":"user"}')`).run(
   279	            `msg_${n}`,
   280	            `ses_${n}`,
   281	            timeMs,
   282	        );
   283	        db.prepare('INSERT INTO part VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)').run(
   284	            `prt_${n}`,
   285	            `msg_${n}`,
   286	            `ses_${n}`,
   287	            timeMs,
   288	            JSON.stringify({
   289	                type: 'file',
   290	                mime: 'image/png',
   291	                filename: `f${n}.png`,
   292	                url: `data:image/png;base64,${Buffer.from(payload).toString('base64')}`,
   293	            }),
   294	        );
   295	    }
   296	
   297	    function withHome<T>(home: string, run: () => T): T {
   298	        const realHome = process.env.HOME;
   299	        process.env.HOME = home;
   300	        try {

 succeeded in 0ms:
   301	            return run();
   302	        } finally {
   303	            process.env.HOME = realHome;
   304	            fs.rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });
   305	        }
   306	    }
   307	
   308	    it('recovers file parts by directory and by session, reporting the original filename', () => {
   309	        const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-oc-'));
   310	        const cwd = '/tmp/proj';
   311	        const db = openDb(home);
   312	        insertImage(db, 1, 'my-session', path.resolve(cwd), Date.parse('2026-08-03T06:00:00.000Z'), 'oc-image');
   313	        db.close();
   314	
   315	        withHome(home, () => {
   316	            const result = recover({ cwd, outDir: path.join(home, 'out') });
   317	            expect(result.harness).toBe('opencode');
   318	            expect(fs.readFileSync(result.images[0].path).toString()).toBe('oc-image');
   319	            expect(result.images[0].filename).toBe('f1.png');
   320	
   321	            const bySlug = recover({ cwd, session: 'my-session', outDir: path.join(home, 'out') });
   322	            expect(bySlug.harness).toBe('opencode');
   323	        });
   324	    });
   325	
   326	    it('matches sessions across the repo-root/subdirectory gap in both directions', () => {
   327	        // opencode records session.directory where it was launched, but runs
   328	        // bash at the repo root; recovery must survive the mismatch.
   329	        const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-oc-dir-'));
   330	        const root = '/tmp/repo';
   331	        const db = openDb(home);
   332	        insertImage(db, 1, 's-sub', path.join(path.resolve(root), 'assets'), 1_000, 'launched-in-subdir');
   333	        db.close();
   334	
   335	        withHome(home, () => {
   336	            const fromRoot = recover({ cwd: root, outDir: path.join(home, 'out') });
   337	            expect(fromRoot.harness).toBe('opencode');
   338	            expect(fs.readFileSync(fromRoot.images[0].path).toString()).toBe('launched-in-subdir');
   339	
   340	            const fromDeeper = recover({
   341	                cwd: path.join(root, 'assets', 'icons'),
   342	                outDir: path.join(home, 'out'),
   343	            });
   344	            expect(fs.readFileSync(fromDeeper.images[0].path).toString()).toBe('launched-in-subdir');
   345	        });
   346	    });
   347	
   348	    it('scopes recovery to the single session owning the newest image', () => {
   349	        const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-oc-scope-'));
   350	        const cwd = '/tmp/proj';
   351	        const db = openDb(home);
   352	        insertImage(db, 1, 's-old', path.resolve(cwd), 1_000, 'old-session-image');
   353	        insertImage(db, 2, 's-new', path.resolve(cwd), 2_000, 'new-session-image');
   354	        db.close();
   355	
   356	        withHome(home, () => {
   357	            const result = recover({ cwd, count: 5, outDir: path.join(home, 'out') });
   358	            expect(result.images).toHaveLength(1);
   359	            expect(fs.readFileSync(result.images[0].path).toString()).toBe('new-session-image');
   360	        });
   361	    });
   362	
   363	    it('outranks older jsonl images when its part is newest', () => {
   364	        const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-oc2-'));
   365	        const cwd = '/tmp/proj';
   366	        const claudeDir = path.join(home, '.claude', 'projects', '-tmp-proj');
   367	        fs.mkdirSync(claudeDir, { recursive: true });

   371	        );
   372	        const db = openDb(home);
   373	        insertImage(db, 1, 's1', path.resolve(cwd), Date.parse('2026-08-03T09:00:00.000Z'), 'oc-newer');
   374	        db.close();
   375	
   376	        withHome(home, () => {
   377	            const result = recover({ cwd, outDir: path.join(home, 'out') });
   378	            expect(result.harness).toBe('opencode');
   379	            expect(fs.readFileSync(result.images[0].path).toString()).toBe('oc-newer');
   380	        });
   381	    });

   383	
   384	describe('harness detection', () => {
   385	    function psTable(rows: Array<[number, number, string]>): string {
   386	        return rows.map(([pid, ppid, command]) => ` ${pid} ${ppid} ${command}`).join('\n');
   387	    }
   388	
   389	    it('finds the nearest harness ancestor by executable basename', () => {
   390	        const ps = psTable([
   391	            [100, 1, '/usr/local/bin/node --no-warnings /Users/x/.claude/local/claude'],
   392	            [200, 100, '/bin/zsh -c modlens'],
   393	            [300, 200, 'node /Users/x/projects/modlens/dist/main.js recover-paste'],
   394	        ]);
   395	        expect(harnessFromPsTable(ps, 300)).toBe('claude-code');
   396	    });
   397	
   398	    it('resolves nesting to the innermost harness', () => {
   399	        // opencode launched from inside a Claude Code bash: opencode is nearer.
   400	        const ps = psTable([
   401	            [100, 1, 'node /Users/x/.claude/local/claude'],
   402	            [200, 100, '/bin/zsh -c "opencode run ..."'],
   403	            [300, 200, '/Users/x/.cache/opencode/bin/opencode run analyze'],
   404	            [400, 300, '/bin/sh -c modlens'],
   405	            [500, 400, 'node /Users/x/modlens/dist/main.js recover-paste'],
   406	        ]);
   407	        expect(harnessFromPsTable(ps, 500)).toBe('opencode');
   408	    });
   409	
   410	    it('ignores free-text arguments beyond the leading tokens', () => {
   411	        const ps = psTable([
   412	            [100, 1, '/opt/homebrew/bin/fish'],
   413	            [
   414	                200, 100,
   415	                'sometool serve --flag a b c d e f "please check the pi and opencode docs"',
   416	            ],
   417	            [300, 200, 'node dist/main.js recover-paste'],
   418	        ]);
   419	        expect(harnessFromPsTable(ps, 300)).toBeNull();
   420	    });
   421	
   422	    it('returns null for terminals with no harness ancestor', () => {
   423	        const ps = psTable([
   424	            [100, 1, '/opt/homebrew/bin/fish'],
   425	            [300, 100, 'node dist/main.js recover-paste'],
   426	        ]);
   427	        expect(harnessFromPsTable(ps, 300)).toBeNull();
   428	    });
   429	
   430	    it('scopes recovery to the detected harness even when another store has newer images', () => {

   488	    it('auto-targets the exact Claude Code session from the injected env var', () => {
   489	        const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-envsess-'));

   494	        fs.writeFileSync(
   495	            path.join(claudeDir, 'decoy.jsonl'),
   496	            imageLine('decoy-newer', '2026-08-04T09:00:00.000Z'),

   507	        try {
   508	            const result = recoverPastedImages({ cwd, outDir: path.join(home, 'out') });

   511	
   512	            // an env session with no transcript must fall back to scanning
   513	            process.env.CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID = 'gone-sess';
   514	            const fallback = recoverPastedImages({ cwd, outDir: path.join(home, 'out') });
   515	            expect(fs.readFileSync(fallback.images[0].path).toString()).toBe('decoy-newer');
   516	        } finally {
   517	            process.env.HOME = realHome;
   518	            fs.rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });
   519	        }
   520	    });

   523	describe('cross-project safety', () => {
   524	    it('escapes SQL wildcards so a path with _ cannot match another project', () => {
   525	        // LIKE reads _ as "any character", so /tmp/proj_1 used to match projA1.
   526	        expect(escapeLikePattern('/tmp/proj_1')).toBe('/tmp/proj\\_1');
   527	        expect(escapeLikePattern('/tmp/100%/x')).toBe('/tmp/100\\%/x');
   528	        expect(escapeLikePattern('/tmp/plain')).toBe('/tmp/plain');
   529	    });
   530	
   531	    it('rejects a transcript whose recorded cwd belongs to another project', () => {
   532	        // /tmp/project.alpha and /tmp/project-alpha share one Claude slug.
   533	        const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-collide-'));
   534	        const slugDir = path.join(home, '.claude', 'projects', claudeProjectSlug('/tmp/project-alpha'));
   535	        fs.mkdirSync(slugDir, { recursive: true });
   536	        fs.writeFileSync(
   537	            path.join(slugDir, 'other.jsonl'),
   538	            `${JSON.stringify({ cwd: '/tmp/project.alpha' })}\n${imageLine('other-project', '2026-08-05T09:00:00.000Z')}`,
   539	        );
   540	
   541	        const realHome = process.env.HOME;
   542	        process.env.HOME = home;
   543	        process.env.MODLENS_HARNESS = 'none';
   544	        try {
   545	            expect(() =>
   546	                recoverPastedImages({ cwd: '/tmp/project-alpha', outDir: path.join(home, 'out') }),
   547	            ).toThrow(/No pasted images/);
   548	        } finally {
   549	            process.env.HOME = realHome;
   550	            fs.rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });
   551	        }
   552	    });
   553	
   554	    it('keeps recovered images private to this user', () => {
   555	        const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-perm-'));
   556	        const dir = path.join(home, '.claude', 'projects', claudeProjectSlug('/tmp/p'));
   557	        fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   558	        fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'c.jsonl'), imageLine('secret', '2026-08-05T09:00:00.000Z'));
   559	
   560	        const realHome = process.env.HOME;
   561	        process.env.HOME = home;
   562	        process.env.MODLENS_HARNESS = 'none';
   563	        try {
   564	            const outDir = path.join(home, 'out');
   565	            const result = recoverPastedImages({ cwd: '/tmp/p', outDir });
   566	            expect(fs.statSync(result.images[0].path).mode & 0o777).toBe(0o600);
   567	            expect(fs.statSync(outDir).mode & 0o777).toBe(0o700);
   568	        } finally {
   569	            process.env.HOME = realHome;
   570	            fs.rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });
   571	        }
   572	    });
   573	
   574	    it('keeps an unmapped media type instead of relabelling it png', () => {
   575	        const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-mime-'));
   576	        const dir = path.join(home, '.claude', 'projects', claudeProjectSlug('/tmp/p'));
   577	        fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });

     1	// Recover pasted images from agent session storage.
     2	//
     3	// None of the supported harnesses writes pasted images to a regular temp
     4	// file, but all of them persist user messages, image bytes included, locally
     5	// before any gateway-side stripping happens:
     6	//
     7	//   claude    ~/.claude/projects/<slug>/<session>.jsonl
     8	//             line: { timestamp: ISO, message: { role, content: [{ type: "image",
     9	//                     source: { type: "base64", media_type, data } }] } }
    10	//   pi        ~/.pi/agent/sessions/--<encoded-cwd>--/<stamp>_<session>.jsonl
    11	//             line: { type: "message", timestamp: ISO, message: { role,
    12	//                     content: [{ type: "image", data, mimeType }] } }
    13	//   opencode  ~/.local/share/opencode/opencode.db (SQLite, needs node:sqlite,
    14	//             Node 22.5+): part.data = { type: "file", mime, url: dataURL },
    15	//             joined to message (role) and session (directory)
    16	//
    17	// Storage layouts are internal implementation details of those tools, so this
    18	// can break without notice; callers should fall back to asking for a path.

    52	/** Last-resort extension from the media type itself, e.g. image/heic -> heic. */
    53	function extensionFromMediaType(mediaType: string): string {
    54	    const subtype = mediaType.split('/')[1]?.split('+')[0]?.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, '');
    55	    return subtype ? subtype.toLowerCase() : 'bin';
    56	}

    71	/** A located source of pasted images: a JSONL transcript or a SQLite db. */
    72	interface SourceRef {
    73	    harness: string;
    74	    /** Displayable location (file path). */
    75	    location: string;
    76	    /** Extract all user images, oldest to newest. */
    77	    extract: () => ImageBlockRef[];
    78	}
    79	
    80	interface HarnessAdapter {
    81	    name: string;
    82	    /** Best candidate for this cwd with the newest image timestamp (epoch ms). */
    83	    findNewest(cwd: string): { ref: SourceRef; timestamp: number } | null;
    84	    /** Exact session lookup. */
    85	    findSession(cwd: string, sessionId: string): SourceRef | null;
    86	    /** Where this adapter looks, for error messages. */
    87	    describe(cwd: string): string;
    88	}

    90	// ---------- shared JSONL helpers ----------
    91	
    92	/**
    93	 * Slugs are lossy: /tmp/project.alpha and /tmp/project-alpha collapse to the
    94	 * same Claude slug, and Pi has the same problem with separators. Both harnesses
    95	 * record the real cwd inside the transcript, so check it before trusting a
    96	 * directory match.
    97	 */
    98	function transcriptBelongsTo(filePath: string, cwd: string): boolean {
    99	    const wanted = path.resolve(cwd);
   100	    let raw: string;
   101	    try {
   102	        raw = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8');
   103	    } catch {
   104	        return false;
   105	    }
   106	    for (const line of raw.split('\n')) {
   107	        if (!line.includes('"cwd"')) {
   108	            continue;
   109	        }
   110	        try {
   111	            const recorded = (JSON.parse(line) as { cwd?: unknown }).cwd;
   112	            if (typeof recorded === 'string') {
   113	                const resolved = path.resolve(recorded);
   114	                return resolved === wanted || resolved.startsWith(`${wanted}${path.sep}`);
   115	            }
   116	        } catch {
   117	            // keep looking
   118	        }
   119	    }
   120	    // No cwd recorded at all: the slug is the only evidence we have.
   121	    return true;
   122	}
   123	
   124	function forEachJsonLine(filePath: string, visit: (line: unknown) => void): void {
   125	    let raw: string;
   126	    try {
   127	        raw = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8');
   128	    } catch {
   129	        return;
   130	    }
   131	    for (const line of raw.split('\n')) {
   132	        if (!line.includes('"image"')) {
   133	            continue;
   134	        }
   135	        try {
   136	            visit(JSON.parse(line));
   137	        } catch {
   138	            // skip malformed lines
   139	        }
   140	    }
   141	}
   142	
   143	function jsonlSource(
   144	    harness: string,
   145	    filePath: string,
   146	    extractLine: (line: unknown) => ImageBlockRef[],
   147	): SourceRef {
   148	    return {
   149	        harness,
   150	        location: filePath,
   151	        extract: () => {
   152	            const images: ImageBlockRef[] = [];
   153	            forEachJsonLine(filePath, (line) => {
   154	                images.push(...extractLine(line));
   155	            });
   156	            return images;
   157	        },
   158	    };
   159	}
   160	
   161	function newestJsonlTimestamp(
   162	    filePath: string,
   163	    extractLine: (line: unknown) => ImageBlockRef[],
   164	): number | null {
   165	    let latest: number | null = null;
   166	    forEachJsonLine(filePath, (line) => {
   167	        if (extractLine(line).length === 0) {
   168	            return;
   169	        }
   170	        const ts = (line as { timestamp?: unknown }).timestamp;
   171	        const ms = typeof ts === 'string' ? Date.parse(ts) : NaN;
   172	        if (Number.isFinite(ms) && (latest === null || ms > latest)) {
   173	            latest = ms;
   174	        }
   175	    });
   176	    return latest;
   177	}
   178	
   179	function listJsonl(dir: string): string[] {
   180	    try {
   181	        return fs
   182	            .readdirSync(dir)
   183	            .filter((name) => name.endsWith('.jsonl'))
   184	            .map((name) => path.join(dir, name));
   185	    } catch {
   186	        return [];
   187	    }
   188	}
   189	
   190	function jsonlAdapter(options: {
   191	    name: string;
   192	    dirFor: (cwd: string) => string;
   193	    matchesSession: (fileName: string, sessionId: string) => boolean;
   194	    extractLine: (line: unknown) => ImageBlockRef[];
   195	}): HarnessAdapter {
   196	    const { name, dirFor, matchesSession, extractLine } = options;
   197	    return {
   198	        name,
   199	        describe: (cwd) => dirFor(cwd),
   200	        findNewest: (cwd) => {
   201	            let best: { ref: SourceRef; timestamp: number } | null = null;
   202	            for (const file of listJsonl(dirFor(cwd))) {
   203	                if (!transcriptBelongsTo(file, cwd)) {
   204	                    continue;
   205	                }
   206	                const timestamp = newestJsonlTimestamp(file, extractLine);
   207	                if (timestamp !== null && (!best || timestamp > best.timestamp)) {
   208	                    best = { ref: jsonlSource(name, file, extractLine), timestamp };
   209	                }
   210	            }
   211	            return best;
   212	        },
   213	        findSession: (cwd, sessionId) => {
   214	            for (const file of listJsonl(dirFor(cwd))) {
   215	                if (matchesSession(path.basename(file), sessionId) && transcriptBelongsTo(file, cwd)) {
   216	                    return jsonlSource(name, file, extractLine);
   217	                }
   218	            }
   219	            return null;
   220	        },
   221	    };
   222	}

   438	const HARNESS_BY_BASENAME: Record<string, string> = {
   439	    claude: 'claude-code',
   440	    'claude-code': 'claude-code',
   441	    pi: 'pi',
   442	    opencode: 'opencode',
   443	    codex: 'codex',
   444	};
   445	
   446	/** Walk the ppid chain in a `ps -Ao pid=,ppid=,command=` table, nearest first. */
   447	export function harnessFromPsTable(psOutput: string, startPid: number): string | null {
   448	    const table = new Map<number, { ppid: number; command: string }>();
   449	    for (const line of psOutput.split('\n')) {
   450	        const match = /^\s*(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(.+)$/.exec(line);
   451	        if (match) {
   452	            table.set(Number(match[1]), { ppid: Number(match[2]), command: match[3] });
   453	        }
   454	    }
   455	    let pid: number | undefined = table.get(startPid)?.ppid;
   456	    for (let hops = 0; hops < 50 && pid !== undefined && pid > 1; hops++) {
   457	        const proc = table.get(pid);
   458	        if (!proc) {
   459	            return null;
   460	        }
   461	        // Only the executable itself, plus the script path when it is run
   462	        // through a node shim. Scanning further matched plain arguments: a
   463	        // command that merely mentioned "pi" was detected as Pi.
   464	        const tokens = proc.command.trim().split(/\s+/);
   465	        const candidates = [tokens[0]];
   466	        if (/^(node|bun|deno)$/.test(path.basename(tokens[0] ?? ''))) {
   467	            const script = tokens.slice(1).find((token) => !token.startsWith('-'));
   468	            if (script) {
   469	                candidates.push(script);
   470	            }
   471	        }
   472	        for (const token of candidates) {
   473	            const mapped = token ? HARNESS_BY_BASENAME[path.basename(token)] : undefined;
   474	            if (mapped) {
   475	                return mapped;
   476	            }
   477	        }
   478	        pid = proc.ppid;
   479	    }
   480	    return null;
   481	}
   482	
   483	function detectHarness(): string | null {
   484	    const override = process.env.MODLENS_HARNESS;
   485	    if (override) {
   486	        return override === 'none' ? null : override;
   487	    }
   488	    try {
   489	        const ps = childProcess.execFileSync('ps', ['-Ao', 'pid=,ppid=,command='], {
   490	            encoding: 'utf-8',
   491	            maxBuffer: 16 * 1024 * 1024,
   492	        });
   493	        const found = harnessFromPsTable(ps, process.pid);
   494	        if (found) {
   495	            return found;
   496	        }
   497	    } catch {
   498	        // ps unavailable (e.g. Windows): fall through to env fingerprints
   499	    }
   500	    if (process.env.PI_CODING_AGENT) {
   501	        return 'pi';
   502	    }
   503	    if (process.env.CODEX_THREAD_ID || process.env.CODEX_SANDBOX) {
   504	        return 'codex';
   505	    }
   506	    if (process.env.CLAUDECODE || process.env.CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID) {
   507	        return 'claude-code';
   508	    }
   509	    return null;
   510	}
   511	
   512	// ---------- orchestration ----------
   513	
   514	const ADAPTERS: HarnessAdapter[] = [claudeAdapter, piAdapter, opencodeAdapter];
   515	
   516	function sourceForExplicitPath(filePath: string, cwd: string, harness?: string): SourceRef {
   517	    // An explicit --harness is the user telling us the format. Honour it: a
   518	    // copied transcript has no telltale path, and guessing read it as Claude.
   519	    const declared = harness && harness !== 'none' ? harness : undefined;
   520	    if (declared === 'opencode' || (!declared && filePath.endsWith('.db'))) {
   521	        return {
   522	            harness: 'opencode',
   523	            location: filePath,
   524	            extract: () => opencodeImagesFromRows(opencodeQuery(filePath, cwd)),
   525	        };
   526	    }
   527	    if (declared === 'pi' || (!declared && filePath.includes(`${path.sep}.pi${path.sep}`))) {
   528	        return jsonlSource('pi', filePath, piExtractLine);
   529	    }
   530	    return jsonlSource('claude-code', filePath, claudeExtractLine);
   531	}
   532	
   533	export function locateSource(cwd: string, adapters: HarnessAdapter[] = ADAPTERS): SourceRef {
   534	    let best: { ref: SourceRef; timestamp: number } | null = null;
   535	    const blockers: string[] = [];
   536	    for (const adapter of adapters) {
   537	        let candidate: { ref: SourceRef; timestamp: number } | null = null;
   538	        try {
   539	            candidate = adapter.findNewest(cwd);
   540	        } catch (error) {
   541	            // An unreadable store should not block the other harnesses, but a
   542	            // setup problem the user can fix (Node too old for node:sqlite)
   543	            // must not vanish into a bare "no images found".
   544	            blockers.push(`${adapter.name}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
   545	        }
   546	        if (candidate && (!best || candidate.timestamp > best.timestamp)) {
   547	            best = candidate;
   548	        }
   549	    }
   550	    if (!best) {
   551	        const dirs = adapters.map((a) => a.describe(cwd)).join(' , ');
   552	        const blocked = blockers.length > 0 ? `\nBlocked: ${blockers.join(' | ')}` : '';
   553	        throw new Error(
   554	            `No pasted images found in any session storage for this directory (looked in: ${dirs}). The user may not have pasted any, or the storage format changed; ask for a file path instead.${blocked}`,
   555	        );
   556	    }
   557	    return best.ref;
   558	}
   559	
   560	export function sourceForSession(
   561	    cwd: string,
   562	    sessionId: string,
   563	    adapters: HarnessAdapter[] = ADAPTERS,
   564	): SourceRef {
   565	    const blockers: string[] = [];
   566	    for (const adapter of adapters) {
   567	        try {
   568	            const ref = adapter.findSession(cwd, sessionId);
   569	            if (ref) {
   570	                return ref;
   571	            }
   572	        } catch (error) {
   573	            blockers.push(`${adapter.name}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
   574	        }
   575	    }
   576	    const dirs = adapters.map((a) => a.describe(cwd)).join(' , ');
   577	    const blocked = blockers.length > 0 ? `\nBlocked: ${blockers.join(' | ')}` : '';
   578	    throw new Error(
   579	        `No session ${sessionId} with pasted images under this project (looked in: ${dirs}). Check --cwd, or drop --session to auto-locate by newest pasted image.${blocked}`,
   580	    );
   581	}
   582	
   583	export function recoverPastedImages(options: RecoverOptions = {}): RecoverResult {
   584	    const cwd = options.cwd ?? process.cwd();
   585	
   586	    const detected = options.transcript ? null : (options.harness ?? detectHarness());
   587	    if (detected === 'codex') {
   588	        throw new Error(
   589	            'This is a Codex session: pasted images already exist as temp files, and each image tag in the message carries its path. Read the path from the tag instead of running recover-paste.',
   590	        );
   591	    }
   592	    const scoped: string | null = detected && detected !== 'none' ? detected : null;
   593	    if (scoped && !ADAPTERS.some((adapter) => adapter.name === scoped)) {
   594	        throw new Error(
   595	            `Unknown harness "${scoped}". Supported: claude-code, pi, opencode (or none to scan all).`,
   596	        );
   597	    }
   598	    const adapters = scoped ? ADAPTERS.filter((adapter) => adapter.name === scoped) : ADAPTERS;
   599	
   600	    // Claude Code injects the session id into tool environments, which lets us
   601	    // target the exact transcript without the skill relaying anything. It can
   602	    // point at an imageless transcript (e.g. a subagent session), so fall back
   603	    // to scanning instead of failing when it does not pan out.
   604	    let source: SourceRef | null = null;
   605	    if (options.transcript) {
   606	        source = sourceForExplicitPath(options.transcript, cwd, options.harness);
   607	    } else if (options.session) {
   608	        source = sourceForSession(cwd, options.session, adapters);
   609	    } else {
   610	        const envSession =
   611	            detected === 'claude-code' ? process.env.CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID : undefined;
   612	        if (envSession) {
   613	            try {
   614	                source = sourceForSession(cwd, envSession, adapters);
   615	            } catch {
   616	                source = null;
   617	            }
   618	        }
   619	        source ??= locateSource(cwd, adapters);
   620	    }

   251	describe('opencode harness support', async () => {
   252	    const { recoverPastedImages: recover } = await import('./recoverPaste.ts');
   253	    const { createRequire } = await import('module');
   254	    const nodeRequire = createRequire(import.meta.url);
   255	    const { DatabaseSync } = nodeRequire('node:sqlite');
   256	
   257	    function openDb(home: string) {
   258	        const dir = path.join(home, '.local', 'share', 'opencode');
   259	        fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   260	        const db = new DatabaseSync(path.join(dir, 'opencode.db'));
   261	        db.exec(`
   262	            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS session (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, slug TEXT, directory TEXT);
   263	            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS message (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, session_id TEXT, time_created INTEGER, data TEXT);
   264	            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS part (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, message_id TEXT, session_id TEXT, time_created INTEGER, data TEXT);
   265	        `);
   266	        return db;
   267	    }
   268	
   269	    function insertImage(
   270	        db: InstanceType<typeof DatabaseSync>,
   271	        n: number,
   272	        slug: string,
   273	        directory: string,
   274	        timeMs: number,
   275	        payload: string,
   276	    ) {
   277	        db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO session VALUES (?, ?, ?)').run(`ses_${n}`, slug, directory);
   278	        db.prepare(`INSERT INTO message VALUES (?, ?, ?, '{"role":"user"}')`).run(
   279	            `msg_${n}`,
   280	            `ses_${n}`,
   281	            timeMs,
   282	        );
   283	        db.prepare('INSERT INTO part VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)').run(
   284	            `prt_${n}`,
   285	            `msg_${n}`,
   286	            `ses_${n}`,
   287	            timeMs,
   288	            JSON.stringify({
   289	                type: 'file',
   290	                mime: 'image/png',
   291	                filename: `f${n}.png`,
   292	                url: `data:image/png;base64,${Buffer.from(payload).toString('base64')}`,
   293	            }),
   294	        );
   295	    }
   296	
   297	    function withHome<T>(home: string, run: () => T): T {
   298	        const realHome = process.env.HOME;
   299	        process.env.HOME = home;
   300	        try {
   301	            return run();
   302	        } finally {
   303	            process.env.HOME = realHome;
   304	            fs.rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });
   305	        }
   306	    }

   593	    });
   594	
   595	    it('only reads the executable name from process ancestry', () => {
   596	        // A command that merely mentions "pi" in its arguments is not Pi.
   597	        const ps = [
   598	            ' 100 1 /opt/homebrew/bin/fish',
   599	            ' 200 100 sometool serve --note "check the pi docs" pi',
   600	            ' 300 200 node /x/dist/main.js recover-paste',
   601	        ].join('\n');
   602	        expect(harnessFromPsTable(ps, 300)).toBeNull();
   603	
   604	        const real = [
   605	            ' 100 1 /usr/local/bin/node /Users/x/.npm/bin/pi',
   606	            ' 300 100 node /x/dist/main.js recover-paste',
   607	        ].join('\n');
   608	        expect(harnessFromPsTable(real, 300)).toBe('pi');
   609	    });

 succeeded in 0ms:
   620	    }
   621	    const count = Math.max(1, options.count ?? 1);
   622	    const outDir = options.outDir ?? path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-paste');
   623	
   624	    const all = source.extract();
   625	    if (all.length === 0) {
   626	        throw new Error(
   627	            `No pasted images found in ${source.location}. The user may not have pasted any, or the storage format changed; ask for a file path instead.`,
   628	        );
   629	    }
   630	
   631	    // Pasted screenshots can hold anything. On a shared /tmp with the usual
   632	    // umask these landed as 0755/0644, readable by every local user.
   633	    fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
   634	    try {
   635	        fs.chmodSync(outDir, 0o700);
   636	    } catch {
   637	        // best effort on platforms without chmod
   638	    }
   639	    const picked = all.slice(-count);
   640	    const images: RecoveredImage[] = picked.map((image) => {
   641	        const buffer = Buffer.from(image.data, 'base64');
   642	        const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(buffer).digest('hex').slice(0, 8);
   643	        // An unmapped image/* used to be relabelled .png, and downstream tools
   644	        // trust the extension, so they were handed the wrong type.
   645	        const ext = EXT_BY_MIME[image.mediaType] ?? extensionFromMediaType(image.mediaType);
   646	        const filePath = path.join(outDir, `paste-${hash}.${ext}`);
   647	        fs.writeFileSync(filePath, buffer, { mode: 0o600 });
   648	        try {
   649	            // writeFileSync only applies mode when creating, and these names are
   650	            // content hashes, so a file recovered before this fix keeps 0644.
   651	            fs.chmodSync(filePath, 0o600);
   652	        } catch {
   653	            // best effort on platforms without chmod
   654	        }
   655	        const recovered: RecoveredImage = {
   656	            path: filePath,
   657	            mediaType: image.mediaType,
   658	            bytes: buffer.length,
   659	        };
   660	        if (image.filename) {
   661	            recovered.filename = image.filename;
   662	        }
   663	        return recovered;
   664	    });
   665	
   666	    const result: RecoverResult = { harness: source.harness, transcript: source.location, images };
   667	    if (scoped) {
   668	        result.detected = scoped;
   669	    }
   670	    return result;
   671	}

    18	
    19	const program = new Command();
    20	
    21	program
    22	    .name('modlens')
    23	    .description('Plug-in vision for text-only LLMs: image in, structured JSON evidence out')
    24	    .version(__APP_VERSION__);
    25	
    26	program
    27	    .command('analyze', { isDefault: true })
    28	    .description('Analyze an image into structured JSON evidence (default command)')
    29	    .requiredOption('-i, --input <path|url>', 'Input image path or https URL')
    30	    .option('-o, --output <path>', 'Write result JSON to a file')
    31	    .option('-m, --model <name>', 'Provider model name')
    32	    .option('-p, --provider <name>', `Vision provider (${listProviders().join(', ')})`)
    33	    .option('--prompt <text>', 'Extra focus for this image')
    34	    .option('--timeout <ms>', 'Provider timeout in milliseconds', '180000')
    35	    .option('--provider-bin <path>', 'Provider binary path (default: agy)')
    36	    .option('--workdir <path>', 'Working directory for the provider')
    37	    .action(async (options) => {
    38	        try {
    39	            const timeoutMs = Number.parseInt(options.timeout, 10);
    40	            if (!Number.isFinite(timeoutMs) || timeoutMs <= 0) {
    41	                throw new Error('Invalid --timeout. Use a positive integer in milliseconds.');
    42	            }
    43	
    44	            const result = await analyzeImage({
    45	                input: options.input,
    46	                provider: options.provider,
    47	                model: options.model,
    48	                prompt: options.prompt,
    49	                timeoutMs,
    50	                providerBin: options.providerBin,
    51	                workdir: options.workdir,
    52	            });
    53	
    54	            const output = JSON.stringify(result, null, 2);
    55	
    56	            if (options.output) {
    57	                const outputPath = path.resolve(options.output);
    58	                fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(outputPath), { recursive: true });
    59	                fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, output, 'utf-8');
    60	            }
    61	
    62	            process.stdout.write(`${output}\n`);
    63	        } catch (error) {
    64	            process.stderr.write(
    65	                `Error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n`,
    66	            );
    67	            process.exit(1);
    68	        }
    69	    });
    70	
    71	program
    72	    .command('recover-paste')
    73	    .description(
    74	        'Recover images pasted into Claude Code, Pi, or OpenCode from local session storage (they never hit disk otherwise)',
    75	    )
    76	    .option('--count <n>', 'How many recent pasted images to recover', '1')
    77	    .option('--out-dir <path>', 'Directory to write recovered images to')
    78	    .option(

    80	        'Claude Code session id for exact targeting (skills get it via ${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID})',
    81	    )
    82	    .option('--transcript <path>', 'Explicit transcript .jsonl or .db (overrides --session)')
    83	    .option(
    84	        '--harness <name>',
    85	        'Force the storage scope: claude-code, pi, opencode, or none (default: auto-detect via process ancestry and env)',
    86	    )
    87	    .option('--cwd <path>', 'Project directory the image was pasted in', process.cwd())
    88	    .action(async (options) => {
    89	        try {
    90	            const count = Number.parseInt(options.count, 10);
    91	            if (!Number.isFinite(count) || count <= 0) {
    92	                throw new Error('Invalid --count. Use a positive integer.');
    93	            }

   103	        } catch (error) {
   104	            process.stderr.write(
   105	                `Error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n`,
   106	            );
   107	            process.exit(1);
   108	        }
   109	    });
   110

   114

   124	            );
   125	        } catch (error) {
   126	            process.stderr.write(
   127	                `Error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n`,
   128	            );
   129	            process.exit(1);
   130	        }
   131	    });
   132

   136	    .action((key: string, value: string) => {
   137	        try {

   140	        } catch (error) {

    39	    /** Fake provider binary plus a throwaway image to analyze. */
    40	    function fakeProvider(script: string) {
    41	        const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-proc-'));
    42	        const bin = path.join(dir, 'fake-agy');
    43	        fs.writeFileSync(bin, script, { mode: 0o755 });
    44	        const image = path.join(dir, 'image.png');
    45	        fs.writeFileSync(image, 'not-a-real-png');
    46	        cleanups.push(() => fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));
    47	        return { bin, image };
    48	    }

    51	        '{"status":"SUCCESS","structured_output":{"summary":"ok","ocr":{"full_text":""}}}';

    55	        // child's 'close' event never fires and the run used to hang until the
    56	        // timeout killed it (issue #1).

    58	            `#!/bin/sh\necho '${SUCCESS_ENVELOPE}'\nsleep 30 &\nexit 0\n`,
    59	        );

    64	            providerBin: bin,
    65	            timeoutMs: 20_000,
    66	            config: {},
    67	        });
    68	
    69	        expect((result.result as { summary: string }).summary).toBe('ok');
    70	        expect(Date.now() - startedAt).toBeLessThan(10_000);
    71	    }, 30_000);
    72	
    73	    it('still reports a non-zero exit with its stderr', async () => {
    74	        const { bin, image } = fakeProvider(
    75	            '#!/bin/sh\necho "boom" >&2\nsleep 30 &\nexit 3\n',
    76	        );
    77	
    78	        await expect(
    79	            analyzeImage({ input: image, providerBin: bin, timeoutMs: 20_000, config: {} }),
    80	        ).rejects.toThrow(/failed with code 3.*boom/s);
    81	    }, 30_000);

 export function resolveInput(input: string): ResolvedInput {
     const trimmed = input.trim();
     if (!trimmed) {
         throw new Error('Input path is required.');
     }
 
     if (isRemoteSource(trimmed)) {
         return { source: trimmed, kind: 'remote' };
     }
 
     if (/^file:\/\//i.test(trimmed)) {
         const localPath = decodeURI(trimmed.replace(/^file:\/\//i, ''));
         return { source: path.resolve(localPath), kind: 'local' };
     }
 
     return { source: path.resolve(trimmed), kind: 'local' };
 }
 
 function isRemoteSource(value: string): boolean {
     return /^https?:\/\//i.test(value.trim());
 }
 
 function validateInputFile(filePath: string): void {
     if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
         throw new Error(`Input image not found: ${filePath}`);
     }
 
     const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);
     if (!stat.isFile()) {
         throw new Error(`Input is not a file: ${filePath}`);
     }
 }

 export function runCommand(
     providerName: string,
     invocation: ProviderInvocation,
     timeoutMs: number,
     describeFailure?: (context: ProviderFailureContext) => string | null,
 ): Promise<CommandResult> {
+    const runStartedAt = Date.now();
     return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
         const child = spawn(invocation.command, invocation.args, {
             cwd: invocation.cwd,
             stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
         });
 
+        // Decoders keep state across chunks: a multi-byte character split down the
+        // middle used to come out as replacement characters.
+        const outDecoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8');
+        const errDecoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8');
         let stdout = '';
         let stderr = '';
         let timedOut = false;
         let settled = false;
         let drainTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
 
         const timer = setTimeout(() => {
             timedOut = true;
             child.kill('SIGTERM');
         }, timeoutMs);
 
         // 'close' waits for every stdio pipe to close, but agy leaves a
         // language server running that inherited the pipe, so its write end
         // never closes and 'close' never fires (issue #1). Settle on 'exit'
         // plus a drain window instead, and drop the pipes afterwards so the
         // lingering descendant cannot keep this process alive either.
         const settle = (code: number | null) => {
             if (settled) {
                 return;
             }
             settled = true;
             clearTimeout(timer);
             clearTimeout(drainTimer);
             child.stdout?.destroy();
             child.stderr?.destroy();
             child.unref();
 
             if (timedOut) {
                 reject(new Error(`${providerName} provider timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms.`));
                 return;
             }
             if (code !== 0) {
                 // The provider knows what its own error output means; a bare
                 // exit code tells the user nothing actionable (issue #3).
-                const explained = describeFailure?.({ stdout, stderr, code }) ?? null;
+                const explained = describeFailure?.({ stdout, stderr, code, startedAt: runStartedAt }) ?? null;
                 reject(
                     new Error(
                         explained ??
                             `${providerName} provider failed with code ${code}.${stderr ? ` stderr: ${stderr.trim()}` : ''}`,
                     ),
                 );
                 return;
             }
             resolve({ stdout, stderr });
         };
 
         let exitCode: number | null = null;
         let exited = false;
         const restartDrain = () => {
             if (!exited || settled) {
                 return;
             }
             clearTimeout(drainTimer);
             drainTimer = setTimeout(() => settle(exitCode), DRAIN_GRACE_MS);
         };
 
         child.stdout.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {
-            stdout += chunk.toString();
+            stdout += outDecoder.decode(chunk, { stream: true });
             restartDrain();
         });
 
         child.stderr.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {
-            stderr += chunk.toString();
+            stderr += errDecoder.decode(chunk, { stream: true });
             restartDrain();
         });
 
         child.on('error', (error) => {
             if (settled) {
                 return;
             }
             settled = true;
             clearTimeout(timer);
             clearTimeout(drainTimer);
             if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === 'ENOENT') {
+                // spawn reports ENOENT for a missing cwd too, and naming the
+                // wrong cause sent people installing software they already had.
+                const missingCwd = !fs.existsSync(invocation.cwd);

                     new Error(
-                        `Provider CLI not found: ${invocation.command}. Install Antigravity CLI and sign in first.`,
+                        missingCwd
+                            ? `Working directory does not exist: ${invocation.cwd}`
+                            : `Provider CLI not found: ${invocation.command}. Install it and sign in first.`,
                     ),
                 );
                 return;
             }
             reject(error);
         });
 
         child.on('exit', (code) => {
             exitCode = code;
             exited = true;
             restartDrain();
         });
 
         // Normal providers close their pipes right after exiting: settle at once.
         child.on('close', (code) => settle(code));
     });

 /** Set a dotted key like "gemini-api.apiKey" or "provider" and persist with 0600 perms. */

 function parseEnvelope(stdout: string): AgyPrintEnvelope {
     const trimmed = stdout.trim();
     let parsed = tryParseJson(trimmed);
 
     if (parsed === null) {
         const firstBrace = trimmed.indexOf('{');
         const lastBrace = trimmed.lastIndexOf('}');
         if (firstBrace >= 0 && lastBrace > firstBrace) {
             parsed = tryParseJson(trimmed.slice(firstBrace, lastBrace + 1));
         }
     }
 
     if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object') {
         throw new Error('Failed to parse Antigravity CLI JSON output.');
     }
 
     return parsed as AgyPrintEnvelope;
 }
 
 function tryParseJson(text: string): unknown | null {
     try {
         return JSON.parse(text);
     } catch {
         return null;
     }
 }
 
 const SWITCH_HINT = `Or switch to a provider with its own quota and no interactive login:
   modlens config set gemini-api.apiKey <key>   # free key, no card: https://aistudio.google.com
   modlens config set provider gemini-api`;
 
 /**
  * agy exits 1 for auth and quota problems alike, and its own envelope says
  * only "Agent execution terminated due to error." The distinguishing evidence
  * lives in agy's log file, so read it before guessing (issue #3).
  */
 export function describeAntigravityFailure(context: ProviderFailureContext): string | null {
+    // Only lines written after this run started can describe this run. A
+    // two-minute window alone let a previous quota failure, or a concurrent
+    // agy call, misdiagnose an unrelated error.
+    const since = context.startedAt ?? Date.now() - LOG_FRESHNESS_MS;
     let envelope: AgyPrintEnvelope | null = null;
     try {
         envelope = parseEnvelope(context.stdout);
     } catch {
         envelope = null;
     }
     // No agy envelope means agy never ran (missing binary, wrapper, crash), so
     // this is not an agy-level error and its logs say nothing about it.
     if (!envelope) {
         return null;
     }
     const agyError = typeof envelope?.error === 'string' ? envelope.error.trim() : '';
-    const evidence = `${agyError}\n${context.stderr}\n${readRecentAgyLog()}`.toLowerCase();
+    const evidence = `${agyError}\n${context.stderr}\n${readRecentAgyLog(since)}`.toLowerCase();
 
     if (evidence.includes('quota')) {
         return [
             agyError || 'Antigravity CLI reported a quota error.',
             "agy's free tier is one weekly bucket shared by the desktop app, the CLI, and the SDK, and subagents drain it in parallel. Wait for the reset shown above, or use a different provider.",
             SWITCH_HINT,
         ].join('\n\n');
     }
 
     if (
         evidence.includes('not logged into antigravity') ||
         evidence.includes('getting token source') ||
         evidence.includes('keyring') ||
         evidence.includes('failed to read token store')
     ) {
         return [
             'Antigravity CLI cannot read its stored login token.',
             "On Linux this usually means the OS keyring is locked, which is normal for headless sessions (agents, cron, systemd, SSH without a desktop login). agy then reports it as being signed out and tries a browser sign-in that cannot complete without a display. Unlock the keyring, or run modlens from a desktop session, or sign in again with `agy`.",
             SWITCH_HINT,
         ].join('\n\n');
     }
 
     const totalTokens = (envelope?.usage as { total_tokens?: number } | undefined)?.total_tokens;
     if (agyError || totalTokens === 0) {
         return [
             agyError || 'Antigravity CLI exited before doing any work (no tokens consumed).',
             `Usually auth or quota. Check \`agy\` interactively, and look at the newest log in ${agyLogDir()} for the real reason.`,
             SWITCH_HINT,
         ].join('\n\n');
     }
 
     return null;
 }
 
 function agyLogDir(): string {
     return path.join(os.homedir(), '.gemini', 'antigravity-cli', 'log');

     } catch {
         return '';
     }
 }

-            `OpenAI-compatible API returned JSON that does not match the vision schema (missing summary/ocr). Retry, or switch to gemini-api / anthropic for enforced schemas. Got: ${truncate(text)}`,
+            `OpenAI-compatible API returned JSON that does not match the vision schema${missing.length > 0 ? ` (missing: ${missing.join(', ')})` : ''}. Retry, or switch to gemini-api / anthropic for enforced schemas. Got: ${truncate(text)}`,
         );
     }
 
     return {
         result,
         meta: {
             conversationId: null,
             durationSeconds: (Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000,
             usage: payload.usage ?? null,
         },
     };
 }

 /** A located source of pasted images: a JSONL transcript or a SQLite db. */

     /** Displayable location (file path). */

 interface HarnessAdapter {
     name: string;
     /** Best candidate for this cwd with the newest image timestamp (epoch ms). */
     findNewest(cwd: string): { ref: SourceRef; timestamp: number } | null;
     /** Exact session lookup. */
