 // ---------- shared JSONL helpers ----------
 
+/**
+ * Slugs are lossy: /tmp/project.alpha and /tmp/project-alpha collapse to the
+ * same Claude slug, and Pi has the same problem with separators. Both harnesses
+ * record the real cwd inside the transcript, so check it before trusting a
+ * directory match.
+ */
+function transcriptBelongsTo(filePath: string, cwd: string): boolean {
+    const wanted = path.resolve(cwd);
+    let raw: string;
+    try {
+        raw = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8');
+    } catch {
+        return false;
+    }
+    for (const line of raw.split('\n')) {
+        if (!line.includes('"cwd"')) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        try {
+            const recorded = (JSON.parse(line) as { cwd?: unknown }).cwd;
+            if (typeof recorded === 'string') {
+                const resolved = path.resolve(recorded);
+                return resolved === wanted || resolved.startsWith(`${wanted}${path.sep}`);
+            }
+        } catch {
+            // keep looking
+        }
+    }
+    // No cwd recorded at all: the slug is the only evidence we have.
+    return true;
+}
+
 function forEachJsonLine(filePath: string, visit: (line: unknown) => void): void {
     let raw: string;
     try {
         raw = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8');
     } catch {
         return;
     }
     for (const line of raw.split('\n')) {
         if (!line.includes('"image"')) {
             continue;
         }
         try {
             visit(JSON.parse(line));
         } catch {
             // skip malformed lines
         }
     }
 }
 
 function jsonlSource(
     harness: string,
     filePath: string,
     extractLine: (line: unknown) => ImageBlockRef[],
 ): SourceRef {
     return {
         harness,
         location: filePath,
         extract: () => {
             const images: ImageBlockRef[] = [];
             forEachJsonLine(filePath, (line) => {
                 images.push(...extractLine(line));
             });
             return images;
         },
     };
 }
 
 function newestJsonlTimestamp(
     filePath: string,
     extractLine: (line: unknown) => ImageBlockRef[],
 ): number | null {
     let latest: number | null = null;
     forEachJsonLine(filePath, (line) => {
         if (extractLine(line).length === 0) {
             return;
         }
         const ts = (line as { timestamp?: unknown }).timestamp;
         const ms = typeof ts === 'string' ? Date.parse(ts) : NaN;
         if (Number.isFinite(ms) && (latest === null || ms > latest)) {
             latest = ms;
         }
     });
     return latest;
 }
 
 function listJsonl(dir: string): string[] {
     try {
         return fs
             .readdirSync(dir)
             .filter((name) => name.endsWith('.jsonl'))
             .map((name) => path.join(dir, name));
     } catch {
         return [];
     }
 }
 
 function jsonlAdapter(options: {
     name: string;
     dirFor: (cwd: string) => string;
     matchesSession: (fileName: string, sessionId: string) => boolean;

     return {
         name,

         findNewest: (cwd) => {
             let best: { ref: SourceRef; timestamp: number } | null = null;
             for (const file of listJsonl(dirFor(cwd))) {
+                if (!transcriptBelongsTo(file, cwd)) {
+                    continue;
+                }
                 const timestamp = newestJsonlTimestamp(file, extractLine);
                 if (timestamp !== null && (!best || timestamp > best.timestamp)) {
                     best = { ref: jsonlSource(name, file, extractLine), timestamp };
                 }
             }
             return best;
         },
         findSession: (cwd, sessionId) => {
             for (const file of listJsonl(dirFor(cwd))) {

+                if (matchesSession(path.basename(file), sessionId) && transcriptBelongsTo(file, cwd)) {
                     return jsonlSource(name, file, extractLine);
                 }
             }
             return null;
         },
     };
 }

 export function claudeProjectSlug(cwd: string): string {
     return path.resolve(cwd).replace(/[/.]/g, '-');

 function claudeExtractLine(line: unknown): ImageBlockRef[] {
     const message = (line as { message?: { role?: string; content?: unknown } }).message;
     if (message?.role !== 'user' || !Array.isArray(message.content)) {
         return [];
     }
     const images: ImageBlockRef[] = [];
     for (const block of message.content) {
         const source = (block as { type?: string; source?: Record<string, string> })?.source;
         if (
             (block as { type?: string })?.type === 'image' &&
             source?.type === 'base64' &&
             source.data
         ) {
             images.push({ mediaType: source.media_type ?? 'image/png', data: source.data });
         }
     }
     return images;
 }

 function opencodeQuery(dbPath: string, cwd: string, sessionId?: string): SqliteRow[] {

     // keep working on older runtimes.

         throw new Error(
             'Reading opencode storage needs the node:sqlite module (Node 22.5+). Upgrade Node, or pass --transcript/--session for a JSONL-based harness.',
         );
     }
 
     const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath, { readOnly: true });

         // records where the session was launched (possibly a subdirectory), so

+        //

+        // path containing either matched other projects until escaped. And a
+        // session id or slug is not unique across projects, so it narrows the
+        // directory match rather than replacing it.

+            : `AND ${directoryFilter}`;
+        const params = sessionId
+            ? [resolved, escaped, resolved, sessionId, sessionId]
+            : [resolved, escaped, resolved];
         const rows = db
             .prepare(
                 `SELECT part.data AS data, part.time_created AS time_created, part.session_id AS session_id
                  FROM part
                  JOIN message ON message.id = part.message_id
                  JOIN session ON session.id = part.session_id
                  WHERE part.data LIKE '{"type":"file"%'
                    AND json_extract(message.data, '$.role') = 'user'
                    ${sessionFilter}
                  ORDER BY part.time_created ASC`,
             )
             .all(...params) as SqliteRow[];
         return rows;
     } finally {
         db.close();
     }
 }
 
 function opencodeImagesFromRows(rows: SqliteRow[]): ImageBlockRef[] {
     const images: ImageBlockRef[] = [];
     for (const row of rows) {
         try {
             const part = JSON.parse(row.data) as {

                 mime?: string;
                 url?: string;
                 filename?: string;
             };

                 continue;
             }

             if (match) {
                 images.push({ mediaType: part.mime, data: match[1], filename: part.filename });
             }
         } catch {
             // skip malformed parts
         }
     }
     return images;

 // ---------- harness detection ----------
 //
 // Recovery must read the storage of the harness this command is actually
 // running inside; racing every store by timestamp lets one tool's stale
 // sessions hijack another tool's paste. The primary signal is the process
 // ancestry: the nearest known harness among our parent processes is the one
 // that spawned this command, which also resolves nested setups (opencode
 // launched from inside Claude Code) to the innermost harness, the one whose
 // input box received the paste. Env fingerprints are the fallback: Claude Code
 // injects CLAUDECODE/CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID, pi sets PI_CODING_AGENT, codex
 // injects CODEX_THREAD_ID; opencode injects nothing, which is exactly why
 // ancestry comes first.
 
 const HARNESS_BY_BASENAME: Record<string, string> = {
     claude: 'claude-code',
     'claude-code': 'claude-code',
     pi: 'pi',
     opencode: 'opencode',
     codex: 'codex',
 };
 
 /** Walk the ppid chain in a `ps -Ao pid=,ppid=,command=` table, nearest first. */
 export function harnessFromPsTable(psOutput: string, startPid: number): string | null {
     const table = new Map<number, { ppid: number; command: string }>();
     for (const line of psOutput.split('\n')) {
         const match = /^\s*(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(.+)$/.exec(line);
         if (match) {
             table.set(Number(match[1]), { ppid: Number(match[2]), command: match[3] });
         }
     }
     let pid: number | undefined = table.get(startPid)?.ppid;
     for (let hops = 0; hops < 50 && pid !== undefined && pid > 1; hops++) {
         const proc = table.get(pid);
         if (!proc) {
             return null;
         }
-        // Only the first few tokens (executable, node shim flags, script path):
-        // deeper tokens are free-text arguments and would false-positive.
-        for (const token of proc.command.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 8)) {
-            const mapped = HARNESS_BY_BASENAME[path.basename(token)];
+        // Only the executable itself, plus the script path when it is run
+        // through a node shim. Scanning further matched plain arguments: a
+        // command that merely mentioned "pi" was detected as Pi.
+        const tokens = proc.command.trim().split(/\s+/);
+        const candidates = [tokens[0]];
+        if (/^(node|bun|deno)$/.test(path.basename(tokens[0] ?? ''))) {
+            const script = tokens.slice(1).find((token) => !token.startsWith('-'));
+            if (script) {
+                candidates.push(script);
+            }
+        }
+        for (const token of candidates) {
+            const mapped = token ? HARNESS_BY_BASENAME[path.basename(token)] : undefined;
             if (mapped) {
                 return mapped;
             }
         }
         pid = proc.ppid;
     }
     return null;
 }
 
 function detectHarness(): string | null {
     const override = process.env.MODLENS_HARNESS;
     if (override) {
         return override === 'none' ? null : override;
     }
     try {
         const ps = childProcess.execFileSync('ps', ['-Ao', 'pid=,ppid=,command='], {
             encoding: 'utf-8',
             maxBuffer: 16 * 1024 * 1024,
         });
         const found = harnessFromPsTable(ps, process.pid);
         if (found) {
             return found;
         }
     } catch {
         // ps unavailable (e.g. Windows): fall through to env fingerprints
     }
     if (process.env.PI_CODING_AGENT) {
         return 'pi';
     }
     if (process.env.CODEX_THREAD_ID || process.env.CODEX_SANDBOX) {
         return 'codex';
     }
     if (process.env.CLAUDECODE || process.env.CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID) {
         return 'claude-code';
     }
     return null;
 }

 export function recoverPastedImages(options: RecoverOptions = {}): RecoverResult {
     const cwd = options.cwd ?? process.cwd();
 
     const detected = options.transcript ? null : (options.harness ?? detectHarness());
     if (detected === 'codex') {
         throw new Error(
             'This is a Codex session: pasted images already exist as temp files, and each image tag in the message carries its path. Read the path from the tag instead of running recover-paste.',
         );
     }
     const scoped: string | null = detected && detected !== 'none' ? detected : null;
     if (scoped && !ADAPTERS.some((adapter) => adapter.name === scoped)) {
         throw new Error(
             `Unknown harness "${scoped}". Supported: claude-code, pi, opencode (or none to scan all).`,
         );
     }
     const adapters = scoped ? ADAPTERS.filter((adapter) => adapter.name === scoped) : ADAPTERS;
 
     // Claude Code injects the session id into tool environments, which lets us
     // target the exact transcript without the skill relaying anything. It can
     // point at an imageless transcript (e.g. a subagent session), so fall back
     // to scanning instead of failing when it does not pan out.
     let source: SourceRef | null = null;
     if (options.transcript) {
-        source = sourceForExplicitPath(options.transcript);
+        source = sourceForExplicitPath(options.transcript, cwd, options.harness);
     } else if (options.session) {
         source = sourceForSession(cwd, options.session, adapters);
     } else {
         const envSession =
             detected === 'claude-code' ? process.env.CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID : undefined;
         if (envSession) {
             try {
                 source = sourceForSession(cwd, envSession, adapters);
             } catch {
                 source = null;
             }
         }
         source ??= locateSource(cwd, adapters);
     }
     const count = Math.max(1, options.count ?? 1);
     const outDir = options.outDir ?? path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-paste');
 
     const all = source.extract();
     if (all.length === 0) {
         throw new Error(
             `No pasted images found in ${source.location}. The user may not have pasted any, or the storage format changed; ask for a file path instead.`,
         );
     }
 
-    fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });
+    // Pasted screenshots can hold anything. On a shared /tmp with the usual
+    // umask these landed as 0755/0644, readable by every local user.
+    fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
+    try {
+        fs.chmodSync(outDir, 0o700);
+    } catch {
+        // best effort on platforms without chmod
+    }
     const picked = all.slice(-count);
     const images: RecoveredImage[] = picked.map((image) => {
         const buffer = Buffer.from(image.data, 'base64');
         const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(buffer).digest('hex').slice(0, 8);
-        const ext = EXT_BY_MIME[image.mediaType] ?? 'png';
+        // An unmapped image/* used to be relabelled .png, and downstream tools
+        // trust the extension, so they were handed the wrong type.
+        const ext = EXT_BY_MIME[image.mediaType] ?? extensionFromMediaType(image.mediaType);
         const filePath = path.join(outDir, `paste-${hash}.${ext}`);
-        fs.writeFileSync(filePath, buffer);
+        fs.writeFileSync(filePath, buffer, { mode: 0o600 });
         const recovered: RecoveredImage = {
             path: filePath,
             mediaType: image.mediaType,
             bytes: buffer.length,
         };
         if (image.filename) {
             recovered.filename = image.filename;
         }
         return recovered;
     });
 
     const result: RecoverResult = { harness: source.harness, transcript: source.location, images };
     if (scoped) {
         result.detected = scoped;
     }
     return result;
 }

skills/modlens/SKILL.md:72:- None of these harnesses writes pasted images to a regular temp file, but all of them persist user messages locally before any gateway strips them: Claude Code and Pi in session JSONL files (`~/.claude/projects/`, `~/.pi/agent/sessions/`), OpenCode in a SQLite database (`~/.local/share/opencode/opencode.db`, read via node:sqlite, needs Node 22.5+). Run `modlens recover-paste` from the project directory the conversation is happening in (add `--count <n>` for several images). It detects which harness it is running inside (process ancestry, then env fingerprints) and reads ONLY that harness's storage, so another tool's old sessions cannot leak in. In Claude Code it also targets your exact session automatically via the injected CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID; `--session <id>` (e.g. from the ${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID} substitution) is only needed to override.

/bin/zsh -lc "node --experimental-strip-types --input-type=module <<'NODE'
import * as fs from 'node:fs'
import * as os from 'node:os'
import * as path from 'node:path'
import * as crypto from 'node:crypto'
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite'
import { claudeProjectSlug, recoverPastedImages } from './src/recoverPaste.ts'

const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-verify-'))
const results = {}

function makeDb(name) {
  const dbPath = path.join(root, name)
  const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath)
  db.exec("'`
    CREATE TABLE session (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, slug TEXT, directory TEXT);
    CREATE TABLE message (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, session_id TEXT, time_created INTEGER, data TEXT);
    CREATE TABLE part (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, message_id TEXT, session_id TEXT, time_created INTEGER, data TEXT);
  `)
  return { db, dbPath }
}

function insertImage(db, id, slug, directory, payload, time = 1) {
  db.prepare('"'INSERT INTO session VALUES (?, ?, ?)').run('ses-' + id, slug, directory)
  db.prepare('INSERT INTO message VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)').run('msg-' + id, 'ses-' + id, time, JSON.stringify({ role: 'user' }))
  db.prepare('INSERT INTO part VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)').run(
    'part-' + id,
    'msg-' + id,
    'ses-' + id,
    time,
    JSON.stringify({
      type: 'file',
      mime: 'image/png',
      filename: id + '.png',
      url: 'data:image/png;base64,' + Buffer.from(payload).toString('base64')
    })
  )
}

function payloads(result) {
  return result.images.map((image) => fs.readFileSync(image.path, 'utf8'))
}

try {
  {
    const { db, dbPath } = makeDb('reverse-wildcard.db')
    insertImage(db, 'wrong', 's', '/tmp/proj_1', 'WRONG_PROJECT')
    db.close()
    const result = recoverPastedImages({
      transcript: dbPath,
      harness: 'opencode',
      cwd: '/tmp/projA1/sub',
      count: 5,
      outDir: path.join(root, 'out-wildcard')
    })
    results.sql_reverse_wildcard = payloads(result)
  }

  {
    const { db, dbPath } = makeDb('session-scope.db')
    insertImage(db, 'right', 'same-slug', '/tmp/right-project', 'RIGHT', 1)
    insertImage(db, 'wrong', 'same-slug', '/tmp/wrong-project', 'WRONG', 2)
    db.close()
    const bySession = recoverPastedImages({
      transcript: dbPath,
      harness: 'opencode',
      cwd: '/tmp/right-project',
      session: 'same-slug',
      count: 5,
      outDir: path.join(root, 'out-session')
    })
    const byCwd = recoverPastedImages({
      transcript: dbPath,
      harness: 'opencode',
      cwd: '/tmp/right-project',
      count: 5,
      outDir: path.join(root, 'out-cwd')
    })
    results.session_directory_scope = payloads(bySession)
    results.explicit_db_cwd = payloads(byCwd)
  }

  {
    const transcript = path.join(root, 'copied-transcript.jsonl')
    fs.writeFileSync(transcript, JSON.stringify({
      type: 'message',
      timestamp: '2026-08-05T00:00:00.000Z',
      message: {
        role: 'user',
        content: [{ type: 'image', data: Buffer.from('PI').toString('base64'), mimeType: 'image/png' }]
      }
    }))
    const result = recoverPastedImages({
      transcript,
      harness: 'pi',
      outDir: path.join(root, 'out-pi')
    })
    results.explicit_harness = { harness: result.harness, payloads: payloads(result) }
  }

  {
    const oldHome = process.env.HOME
    const oldHarness = process.env.MODLENS_HARNESS
    process.env.HOME = root
    process.env.MODLENS_HARNESS = 'none'
    try {
      const wanted = '/tmp/project-alpha'
      const other = '/tmp/project.alpha'
      const dir = path.join(root, '.claude', 'projects', claudeProjectSlug(wanted))
      fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true })
      const transcript = path.join(dir, 'mixed.jsonl')
      const imageLine = JSON.stringify({
        cwd: other,
        timestamp: '2026-08-05T01:00:00.000Z',
        message: {
          role: 'user',
          content: [{
            type: 'image',
            source: {
              type: 'base64',
              media_type: 'image/png',
              data: Buffer.from('OTHER_PROJECT_IMAGE').toString('base64')
            }
          }]
        }
      })
      fs.writeFileSync(transcript, JSON.stringify({ cwd: wanted }) + '\\n' + imageLine)
      const result = recoverPastedImages({
        cwd: wanted,
        outDir: path.join(root, 'out-mixed')
      })
      results.first_cwd_only = payloads(result)
    } finally {
      if (oldHome === undefined) delete process.env.HOME
      else process.env.HOME = oldHome
      if (oldHarness === undefined) delete process.env.MODLENS_HARNESS
      else process.env.MODLENS_HARNESS = oldHarness
    }
  }

  {
    const bytes = Buffer.from('PRIVATE_HEIC')
    const transcript = path.join(root, 'private.jsonl')
    fs.writeFileSync(transcript, JSON.stringify({
      timestamp: '2026-08-05T02:00:00.000Z',
      message: {
        role: 'user',
        content: [{
          type: 'image',
          source: {
            type: 'base64',
            media_type: 'image/heic',
            data: bytes.toString('base64')
          }
        }]
      }
    }))
    const outDir = path.join(root, 'preexisting-out')
    fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { mode: 0o755 })
    fs.chmodSync(outDir, 0o755)
    const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex').slice(0, 8)
    const oldPath = path.join(outDir, "'`paste-${hash}.heic`)
    fs.writeFileSync(oldPath, bytes, { mode: 0o644 })
    fs.chmodSync(oldPath, 0o644)
    const result = recoverPastedImages({ transcript, outDir })
    results.permissions_and_extension = {
      dir: (fs.statSync(outDir).mode & 0o777).toString(8),
      file: (fs.statSync(result.images[0].path).mode & 0o777).toString(8),
      extension: path.extname(result.images[0].path)
    }
  }

  console.log(JSON.stringify(results, null, 2))
} finally {
  fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true })
}

import * as fs from 'node:fs'
import * as os from 'node:os'
import * as path from 'node:path'
import { analyzeImage, runCommand } from './src/analyzer.ts'

const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-proc-verify-'))
const results = {}

async function capture(name, task) {
  const started = Date.now()
  try {
    const value = await task()
    results[name] = { status: 'resolved', elapsedMs: Date.now() - started, value }
  } catch (error) {
    results[name] = { status: 'rejected', elapsedMs: Date.now() - started, message: error.message }
  }
}

try {
  await capture('successful_exit_open_pipe', () =>
    runCommand('probe', {
      command: process.execPath,
      args: ['-e', "'`
        const { spawn } = require('"'node:child_process')
        process.stdout.write('OK')
        const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['-e', 'setTimeout(() => {}, 1000)'], {
          stdio: ['ignore', 'inherit', 'inherit']
        })
        child.unref()
      "'`],
      cwd: root
    }, 100)
  )

  await capture('"'split_utf8', () =>
    runCommand('probe', {
      command: process.execPath,
      args: ['-e', "'`
        process.stdout.write(Buffer.from([0x41, 0xf0, 0x9f]))
        setTimeout(() => {
          process.stdout.write(Buffer.from([0x99, 0x82, 0x42]))
        }, 30)
      `],
      cwd: root
    }, 1000)
  )

  const pidFile = path.join(root, '"'stubborn.pid')
  const externalKill = setTimeout(() => {
    try {
      const pid = Number(fs.readFileSync(pidFile, 'utf8'))
      process.kill(pid, 'SIGKILL')
    } catch {}
  }, 2700)
  await capture('stubborn_timeout', () =>
    runCommand('probe', {
      command: process.execPath,
      args: ['-e', "'`
        require('"'node:fs').writeFileSync("'${JSON.stringify(pidFile)}, String(process.pid))
        process.on('"'SIGTERM', () => {})
        setInterval(() => {}, 1000)
      "'`],
      cwd: root
    }, 50)
  )
  clearTimeout(externalKill)

  await capture('"'missing_binary', () =>
    runCommand('probe', {
      command: path.join(root, 'does-not-exist'),
      args: [],
      cwd: root
    }, 1000)
  )

  await capture('missing_cwd', () =>
    runCommand('probe', {
      command: process.execPath,
      args: ['-e', ''],
      cwd: path.join(root, 'does-not-exist')
    }, 1000)
  )

  const fakeClaude = path.join(root, 'fake-claude')
  const image = path.join(root, 'image.png')
  fs.writeFileSync(fakeClaude, '#"'!/bin/sh'"\\nexec sleep 5\\n', { mode: 0o755 })
  fs.writeFileSync(image, 'x')
  await capture('claude_timeout_backstop', () =>
    analyzeImage({
      input: image,
      provider: 'claude-cli',
      providerBin: fakeClaude,
      timeoutMs: 100,
      config: {}
    })
  )

  if (results.split_utf8.status === 'resolved') {
    results.split_utf8.value = results.split_utf8.value.stdout
  }
  console.log(JSON.stringify(results, null, 2))
} finally {
  fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true })
}

      settings: resolveProviderSettings(canonical, {
        providers: { [alias]: { apiKey: alias + '-key', model: alias + '-model' } }
      }, {})
    }
  }

  const configPath = path.join(root, 'config.json')
  fs.writeFileSync(configPath, '{\"provider\":\"gemini-api\"}')
  fs.chmodSync(configPath, 0o000)

    results.unreadable_config = { status: 'resolved', value: loadConfigFile(configPath) }
  } catch (error) {
    results.unreadable_config = { status: 'threw', message: error.message }
  } finally {
    fs.chmodSync(configPath, 0o600)
  }

          visual: {},

    })
    results.nested_partial_schema = { status: 'resolved', result: value.result }
  } catch (error) {
    results.nested_partial_schema = { status: 'rejected', message: error.message }
  }

  fs.mkdirSync(logDir, { recursive: true })
  const logPath = path.join(logDir, 'daily.log')

  const startedAt = Date.now()
  fs.appendFileSync(logPath, 'new run: unrelated failure\\n')

  fs.utimesSync(logPath, future, future)

      usage: { total_tokens: 0 }
    }),
    stderr: '',

} finally {

  if (oldHome === undefined) delete process.env.HOME

import * as fs from 'node:fs'
import * as os from 'node:os'
import * as path from 'node:path'
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite'
import { recoverPastedImages } from './src/recoverPaste.ts'

const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'modlens-session-verify-'))
const oldHome = process.env.HOME
const dbDir = path.join(home, '.local', 'share', 'opencode')
fs.mkdirSync(dbDir, { recursive: true })
const db = new DatabaseSync(path.join(dbDir, 'opencode.db'))
db.exec("'`
  CREATE TABLE session (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, slug TEXT, directory TEXT);
  CREATE TABLE message (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, session_id TEXT, time_created INTEGER, data TEXT);
  CREATE TABLE part (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, message_id TEXT, session_id TEXT, time_created INTEGER, data TEXT);
`)
function add(id, directory, payload) {
  db.prepare('"'INSERT INTO session VALUES (?, ?, ?)').run(id, 'same-slug', directory)
  db.prepare('INSERT INTO message VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)').run('m-' + id, id, 1, '{\"role\":\"user\"}')
  db.prepare('INSERT INTO part VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)').run(
    'p-' + id,
    'm-' + id,
    id,
    1,
    JSON.stringify({
      type: 'file',
      mime: 'image/png',
      url: 'data:image/png;base64,' + Buffer.from(payload).toString('base64')
    })
  )
}
add('right', '/tmp/right-project', 'RIGHT')
add('wrong', '/tmp/wrong-project', 'WRONG')
db.close()
process.env.HOME = home
try {
  const result = recoverPastedImages({
    cwd: '/tmp/right-project',
    session: 'same-slug',
    harness: 'opencode',

     1	# Changelog
     2	
     3	## 2.7.4 - 2026-08-05
     4	
     5	Correctness and privacy pass after an external review (gpt-5.6-sol) that proved every finding with a probe.
     6	
     7	**Recovering the wrong project's images**
     8	
     9	- OpenCode directory matching passed the project path straight into SQL `LIKE`, where `_` and `%` are wildcards, so a path containing either matched other projects. Patterns are escaped now.
    10	- `--session <id>` dropped the directory condition entirely, and session slugs are not unique across projects. The reviewer found two colliding slugs in a real local database. A session now narrows the directory match instead of replacing it.
    11	- Claude Code and Pi directory slugs are lossy: `/tmp/project.alpha` and `/tmp/project-alpha` produce the same slug. Both harnesses record the real cwd inside the transcript, which is now checked before a transcript is trusted.
    12	
    13	**Privacy**
    14	
    15	- Recovered images landed as 0644 inside a 0755 directory, so on a shared `/tmp` any local user could read them. They are written 0600 into a 0700 directory, and re-chmodded because the filenames are content hashes and an existing file keeps its old mode.
    16	
    17	**Correctness**
    18	
    19	- A successful run could be reported as a timeout: the timer stayed armed while output drained, so a slow drain turned exit code 0 into a timeout error. It is cleared when the child exits.
    20	- A timeout sent one SIGTERM and then waited, so an engine ignoring signals hung the CLI. It now settles immediately and escalates to SIGKILL.
    21	- Output decoding kept no state across chunks, so a multi-byte character split across a chunk boundary became replacement characters.
    22	- The OpenCode "needs Node 22.5" message was swallowed by an empty catch, leaving only "no pasted images". Setup problems now travel with the error.
    23	- `--harness` was ignored when `--transcript` was given, so a copied Pi transcript was parsed as Claude Code. `--transcript <db>` also ignored `--cwd`.
    24	- Harness detection scanned the first eight command tokens, so a command that merely mentioned "pi" in its arguments was detected as Pi. Only the executable, plus the script path behind a node shim, is read now.
    25	- agy log evidence was accepted if the file was under two minutes old, which let a previous quota failure or a concurrent agy call misdiagnose an unrelated error. Evidence must now postdate the start of this run.
    26	- The `claude-cli` provider inherited a 30 second kill grace meant for agy's own `--print-timeout`, silently extending `--timeout`. The grace applies only to engines with an internal deadline.
    27	- The openai provider's "schema validation" accepted `{"summary":"x","ocr":null}` and anything missing layout, semantics, visual, or uncertainty. All required fields are checked.
    28	- Settings saved under a provider alias (`config set gemini.apiKey`) were invisible once the name resolved to `gemini-api`.
    29	- An unmapped image type was relabelled `.png`, so downstream tools reading the extension got the wrong type.
    30	- `ENOENT` from spawn was always reported as a missing CLI, even when the real cause was a missing working directory.
    31	- A config file that exists but cannot be read (permissions) silently became an empty config.
    32	
    33	## 2.7.3 - 2026-08-05
    34	
    35	- Fix: a failing `antigravity-cli` run now explains itself instead of reporting a bare exit code ([#3](https://github.com/liustack/modlens/issues/3), thanks @mtongle). Providers gained a `describeFailure` hook, and the agy provider uses it to surface agy's own error text and classify the two failures users actually hit: a locked OS keyring in headless sessions (the report's case, where agy claims to be signed out) and an exhausted weekly quota. Both messages end with the exact commands to switch to a keyless, quota-independent provider. Diagnosis only reads agy's log when this run produced an agy error envelope and the log is fresh, so stale logs cannot misdiagnose an unrelated failure.
    36	- Docs: README leads with paste support, recommends the free AI Studio key over the slower agy default, and documents that the skill configures modlens for you on request.
    37	
    38	## 2.7.2 - 2026-08-05
    39	
    40	- Fix: runs with the `antigravity-cli` provider hung until the timeout killed them ([#1](https://github.com/liustack/modlens/issues/1), thanks @hawkmor for the diagnosis). agy exits cleanly but leaves a language server holding the inherited stdout pipe, so the child's `close` event never fires. The provider run now settles on `exit` plus a short drain window, and releases the pipes afterwards so a lingering descendant cannot keep the CLI process alive either.
    41	
    42	## 2.7.1 - 2026-08-04
    43	
    44	- Docs: per-harness skill discovery paths (`~/.claude/skills/`, `~/.codex/skills/`, `~/.agents/skills/` for Pi and OpenCode), OpenCode + DeepSeek setup recipe, and the four-harness live verification matrix (Claude Code session-id recovery, OpenCode full skill loop on DeepSeek, Pi store isolation, Codex refusal).
    45	
    46	## 2.7.0 - 2026-08-04
    47	
    48	- `recover-paste` now identifies the harness it is running inside before touching any storage: process ancestry first (the nearest known harness among parent processes, which also resolves nested setups to the innermost tool), env fingerprints second (`CLAUDECODE`, `PI_CODING_AGENT`, `CODEX_THREAD_ID`). Detection scopes recovery to that harness's store only, so another tool's stale sessions can never hijack a paste; Codex is refused outright with path-tag guidance. `--harness <name|none>` overrides, output gains a `detected` field.
    49	- In Claude Code, recovery auto-targets the exact session from the injected `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID`, falling back to newest-image scanning when that transcript holds no images (subagent sessions).

    93	        result,

    95	            conversationId: null,
    96	            durationSeconds: (Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000,
    97	            usage: payload.usage ?? null,

   150	): Promise<CommandResult> {
   151	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   152	        const child = spawn(invocation.command, invocation.args, {
   153	            cwd: invocation.cwd,
   154	            stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
   155	        });
   156	
   157	        let stdout = '';
   158	        let stderr = '';
   159	        let timedOut = false;
   160	        let settled = false;
   161	        let drainTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
   162	
   163	        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
   164	            timedOut = true;
   165	            child.kill('SIGTERM');
   166	        }, timeoutMs);
   167	
   168	        // 'close' waits for every stdio pipe to close, but agy leaves a
   169	        // language server running that inherited the pipe, so its write end
   170	        // never closes and 'close' never fires (issue #1). Settle on 'exit'
   171	        // plus a drain window instead, and drop the pipes afterwards so the
   172	        // lingering descendant cannot keep this process alive either.
   173	        const settle = (code: number | null) => {
   174	            if (settled) {
   175	                return;
   176	            }
   177	            settled = true;
   178	            clearTimeout(timer);
   179	            clearTimeout(drainTimer);
   180	            child.stdout?.destroy();
   181	            child.stderr?.destroy();
   182	            child.unref();
   183	
   184	            if (timedOut) {
   185	                reject(new Error(`${providerName} provider timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms.`));
   186	                return;
   187	            }
   188	            if (code !== 0) {
   189	                // The provider knows what its own error output means; a bare
   190	                // exit code tells the user nothing actionable (issue #3).
   191	                const explained = describeFailure?.({ stdout, stderr, code }) ?? null;
   192	                reject(
   193	                    new Error(
   194	                        explained ??
   195	                            `${providerName} provider failed with code ${code}.${stderr ? ` stderr: ${stderr.trim()}` : ''}`,
   196	                    ),
   197	                );
   198	                return;
   199	            }
   200	            resolve({ stdout, stderr });
   201	        };
   202	
   203	        let exitCode: number | null = null;
   204	        let exited = false;
   205	        const restartDrain = () => {
   206	            if (!exited || settled) {
   207	                return;
   208	            }
   209	            clearTimeout(drainTimer);
   210	            drainTimer = setTimeout(() => settle(exitCode), DRAIN_GRACE_MS);
   211	        };
   212	
   213	        child.stdout.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {
   214	            stdout += chunk.toString();
   215	            restartDrain();
   216	        });
   217	
   218	        child.stderr.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {
   219	            stderr += chunk.toString();
   220	            restartDrain();
   221	        });
   222	
   223	        child.on('error', (error) => {
   224	            if (settled) {
   225	                return;
   226	            }
   227	            settled = true;
   228	            clearTimeout(timer);
   229	            clearTimeout(drainTimer);
   230	            if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === 'ENOENT') {
   231	                reject(
   232	                    new Error(
   233	                        `Provider CLI not found: ${invocation.command}. Install Antigravity CLI and sign in first.`,
   234	                    ),
   235	                );
   236	                return;
   237	            }
   238	            reject(error);
   239	        });
   240	
   241	        child.on('exit', (code) => {
   242	            exitCode = code;
   243	            exited = true;
   244	            restartDrain();
   245	        });
   246	
   247	        // Normal providers close their pipes right after exiting: settle at once.
   248	        child.on('close', (code) => settle(code));
   249	    });
   250	}

codex
`pnpm typecheck` 与全部 80 个测试均通过，但隔离探针确认仍有 7 项未修好。

1. **STILL BROKEN**。只转义了正向 `LIKE`。反向条件仍把 `session.directory` 当作未转义模式。[recoverPaste.ts:330](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/recoverPaste.ts:330)。探针以 cwd `/tmp/projA1/sub` 查询时，错误恢复了目录 `/tmp/proj_1` 的图片。

2. **CONFIRMED FIXED**。session 条件现在与目录条件通过 `AND` 组合。[recoverPaste.ts:332](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/recoverPaste.ts:332)。两个项目使用相同 slug 的探针只返回正确项目。

3. **CONFIRMED FIXED**（单一 cwd transcript）。Claude 与 Pi 的自动扫描和 session 查找均调用 `transcriptBelongsTo`。[recoverPaste.ts:203](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/recoverPaste.ts:203)。多 cwd 缺陷见 NEW BUGS。

4. **CONFIRMED FIXED**。目录创建后强制 chmod 0700，文件写入后强制 chmod 0600。[recoverPaste.ts:631](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/recoverPaste.ts:631)。预先存在的 0755 目录与 0644 文件被修正为 0700 和 0600。

5. **STILL BROKEN**。timeout 只在 `settle` 中清除，`exit` 事件没有清除。[analyzer.ts:184](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/analyzer.ts:184) [analyzer.ts:257](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/analyzer.ts:257)。成功进程退出后由后代保持 stdout，100ms timeout 仍在 538ms 后被误报。

6. **STILL BROKEN**。`SIGKILL_GRACE_MS` 只声明未使用。[analyzer.ts:58](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/analyzer.ts:58)。忽略 SIGTERM 的进程一直挂起，直到探针在 2.7 秒后外部发送 SIGKILL，Promise 才结束。

7. **CONFIRMED FIXED**。stdout 与 stderr 使用流式 `TextDecoder`。[analyzer.ts:164](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/analyzer.ts:164)。跨 chunk 拆开的 emoji 正确得到 `A🙂B`。

8. **CONFIRMED FIXED**。适配器错误被加入 `Blocked` 信息。[recoverPaste.ts:535](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/recoverPaste.ts:535)。禁用 `node:sqlite` 后，Node 22.5+ 提示成功到达用户。

9. **CONFIRMED FIXED**。显式 transcript 会使用 `--harness`，数据库查询会使用传入 cwd。[recoverPaste.ts:516](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/recoverPaste.ts:516)。复制后的 Pi transcript 和显式数据库 cwd 探针均正确。

10. **STILL BROKEN**。Node shim 通过“第一个非选项 token”猜脚本路径。[recoverPaste.ts:464](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/recoverPaste.ts:464)。`node --require pi /tmp/unrelated.js` 被错误识别成 Pi，因为 `pi` 实际是选项参数。

11. **STILL BROKEN**。这里只检查最新日志文件的 mtime，并未绑定本次进程或过滤文件内旧证据。[antigravity.ts:187](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/providers/antigravity.ts:187)。并发运行产生更新的 quota 日志时，当前运行仍被误诊为 quota。

12. **CONFIRMED FIXED**。30 秒宽限只用于声明 `hasInternalTimeout` 的 provider。[analyzer.ts:88](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/analyzer.ts:88)。Claude CLI 的 100ms timeout 在约 105ms 返回。

13. **STILL BROKEN**。只检查顶层字段，以及 `summary`、`ocr`、`uncertainty` 的少量类型。[openaiCompat.ts:87](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/providers/openaiCompat.ts:87)。`ocr: {}`、`layout: {}`、`semantics: {}` 等缺少嵌套必填字段的结果仍被接受。

14. **CONFIRMED FIXED**。`gemini` 配置会合并到 `gemini-api`。[config.ts:68](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/config.ts:68)。探针成功读取 alias 下保存的 apiKey 与 model。

15. **CONFIRMED FIXED**。未知 MIME subtype 会生成自身扩展名。[recoverPaste.ts:52](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/recoverPaste.ts:52)。`image/heic` 输出 `.heic`。

16. **CONFIRMED FIXED**。ENOENT 会先检查 cwd 是否存在。[analyzer.ts:241](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/analyzer.ts:241)。缺 binary 与缺 cwd 得到不同且正确的错误。

17. **STILL BROKEN**。读取配置的 catch 仍无条件返回空对象。[config.ts:27](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/config.ts:27)。chmod 000 的现有配置文件被静默解析成 `{}`。

## NEW BUGS

- `transcriptBelongsTo` 在第一条 cwd 后立即返回。[recoverPaste.ts:98](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/recoverPaste.ts:98)。探针中第一条 cwd 属于目标项目，后续图片行属于碰撞项目，错误图片仍被恢复。它还会同步完整读取每个 transcript，随后图片扫描再次完整读取，造成大 transcript 的双倍 I/O 与内存开销。

- 新 alias 表与真实 provider alias 不一致。`claude` 实际解析为 `anthropic`，配置表却映射到 `claude-cli`，同时漏掉 `claude-code` 和 `openai-compat`。[config.ts:54](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/config.ts:54) [providers/index.ts:57](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/providers/index.ts:57)。这会把 Claude API 模型配置错误应用给 Claude CLI，并继续丢失另外两个 alias 的配置。

- `--transcript` 会跳过 harness 合法性校验。[recoverPaste.ts:586](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/recoverPaste.ts:586)。探针传入 `--harness bogus` 或 `--harness codex` 均未报错，反而静默按 Claude transcript 解析。

项目文件未修改，工作区保持干净。

- 新 alias 表与真实 provider alias 不一致。`claude` 实际解析为 `anthropic`，配置表却映射到 `claude-cli`，同时漏掉 `claude-code` 和 `openai-compat`。[config.ts:54](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/config.ts:54) [providers/index.ts:57](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/providers/index.ts:57)。这会把 Claude API 模型配置错误应用给 Claude CLI，并继续丢失另外两个 alias 的配置。

- `--transcript` 会跳过 harness 合法性校验。[recoverPaste.ts:586](/Users/leon/projects/modlens/src/recoverPaste.ts:586)。探针传入 `--harness bogus` 或 `--harness codex` 均未报错，反而静默按 Claude transcript 解析。
